				<title><![CDATA[Samsung Galaxy Buds4 Pro Superior Hi-Fi Sound Recognized by Audio Experts]]></title>
				<link>https://news.samsung.com/global/samsung-galaxy-buds4-pro-superior-hi-fi-sound-recognized-by-audio-experts</link>
				<pubDate>Mon, 24 Aug 2026 07:00:00 +0000</pubDate>
								<media:content url="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/08/21093103/Samsung-Mobile-Galaxy-Buds4-Pro-EISA-Recognition_Thumb932-728x410.jpg" medium="image" />
				<dc:creator><![CDATA[Samsung Newsroom]]></dc:creator>

									<description><![CDATA[Samsung Electronics today announced that Galaxy Buds4 Pro has received the EISA In-Ear Headphones 2026-2027 Award by the Expert Imaging and Sound Association (EISA). This marks the first time a Galaxy Buds product has earned this recognition from EISA, underscoring Samsung’s continued commitment to audio innovation, superior sound quality, intelligent adaptive listening technologies, and user-focused […]]]></description>
																<content:encoded><![CDATA[
<p>Samsung Electronics today announced that Galaxy Buds4 Pro has received the EISA In-Ear Headphones 2026-2027 Award by the Expert Imaging and Sound Association (EISA). This marks the first time a Galaxy Buds product has earned this recognition from EISA, underscoring Samsung’s continued commitment to audio innovation, superior sound quality, intelligent adaptive listening technologies, and user-focused design.</p>



<p>The award is particularly significant because it validates the core engineering philosophy behind Galaxy Buds4 Pro: delivering sound as it was meant to be heard. The EISA Awards represent one of the most authoritative annual recognitions of audio quality in the global consumer electronics industry. Judged by a collective of expert editors from top technology publications across more than 25 countries, these accolades provide an independent assessment of audio performance and innovation. The recognition reinforces Samsung’s ongoing efforts to push the boundaries of premium wireless audio through advanced hardware, intelligent software, and user-centric design.</p>



<p>EISA’s praise further highlights the Galaxy Buds4 Pro’s balance of audio performance, call quality and everyday usability.</p>



<p>“Employing a two-way speaker with individual amplifiers dedicated to the woofer and tweeter Samsung’s Galaxy Buds4 Pro yields clean, musical detail,” commented Ljubisa Miodragovic, President at EISA. “Meanwhile, advanced Sensor Fusion technology combines a dedicated Voice Pickup Unit (VPU) with DNN-powered noise reduction to ensure exceptionally clear calls.”</p>



<p>“At Samsung, our goal has always been to deliver Hi-Fi sound that stays true to the original recording. Receiving the EISA In-Ear Headphones Award validates our commitment to audio engineering excellence and affirms our promise of delivering premium sound quality and user experiences,” said Han-gil Moon, Master and Head of Audio Group, Mobile eXperience (MX) Business at Samsung Electronics. “Users can be confident that Galaxy Buds4 Pro are built to provide the best and most satisfying sound experience. We will continue to build on this foundation to offer even richer listening experiences in the future.”</p>



<p>Galaxy Buds4 Pro delivers Samsung’s most advanced audio experience to date, with standout features including:</p>



<ul class="wp-block-list">
<li><strong>A newly designed wider woofer </strong>that increases the effective speaker area by nearly 20% over the previous generation, paired with a dedicated tweeter to deliver clean bass, rich treble, and 24-bit/96 kHz UHQ audio true to the original recording. EISA experts praised Buds4 Pro for yielding “clean musical detail while limiting distortion across the frequency range.”</li>



<li><strong>Enhanced Adaptive ANC and EQ </strong>that analyzes wearing conditions and each user’s unique ear shape, applying optimal noise-cancelling algorithms in real time, so users can enjoy a consistent listening experience all day long.</li>



<li><strong>Super Clear Call </strong>enabled by the device’s advanced Sensor Fusion technology, which combines a Voice Pickup Unit (VPU) with Deep Neural Network (DNN)-powered noise reduction to “ensure exceptionally clear calls,” even in noisy environments.</li>



<li><strong>An ultra-sleek, ergonomic blade design </strong>refined through hundreds of millions of global ear data points and over 10,000 simulations, featuring a premium metal finish, engraved pinch controls, and an IP57 rating for water and dust resistance.<sup data-fn="89c45d71-f28b-4b9a-bdf4-f538748f1660" class="fn"><a href="#89c45d71-f28b-4b9a-bdf4-f538748f1660" id="89c45d71-f28b-4b9a-bdf4-f538748f1660-link">1</a></sup></li>



<li><strong>Deeper Galaxy ecosystem integration</strong> that allows users to initiate AI agents and manage hands-free calls through intuitive <strong>Head Gesture</strong> controls.</li>
</ul>



<p>To learn more about the Galaxy Buds4 series, please visit: <a href="http://www.samsung.com/galaxy-buds4-series/" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer noopener">www.samsung.com/galaxy-buds4-series/</a>.</p>



<hr class="wp-block-separator has-alpha-channel-opacity" />


<ol class="wp-block-footnotes"><li id="89c45d71-f28b-4b9a-bdf4-f538748f1660">IP rated for submersion in up to 3 feet of fresh water for up to 30 minutes (charging case is not water resistant). Not advised for beach or pool use. If the earbuds or user’s hands are wet, they must be dried thoroughly prior to handling. Damaged earbuds are not guaranteed to be water resistant. Earbuds only. <a href="#89c45d71-f28b-4b9a-bdf4-f538748f1660-link" aria-label="Jump to footnote reference 1"><img src="https://s.w.org/images/core/emoji/16.0.1/72x72/21a9.png" alt="↩" class="wp-smiley" style="height: 1em; max-height: 1em;" />︎</a></li></ol>]]></content:encoded>

				<title><![CDATA[Samsung Electronics To Implement Largest-Ever Shareholder Return in 2026, Estimated at KRW 90 to 110 Trillion]]></title>
				<link>https://news.samsung.com/global/samsung-electronics-to-implement-largest-ever-shareholder-return-in-2026-estimated-at-krw-90-to-110-trillion</link>
				<pubDate>Fri, 21 Aug 2026 18:27:19 +0000</pubDate>
								<media:content url="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/08/21182520/Samsung-Corporate-More-Stories-2026-Shareholder_Thumb932-728x410.jpg" medium="image" />
				<dc:creator><![CDATA[Samsung Newsroom]]></dc:creator>
						<category><![CDATA[More Stories]]></category>
		<category><![CDATA[Press Releases]]></category>
                <guid isPermaLink="false">https://bit.ly/4cMFSMT</guid>
									<description><![CDATA[Samsung Electronics’ Board of Directors convened today and approved a shareholder return plan for 2026, estimated at approximately KRW 90 trillion to KRW 110 trillion. This is approximately five times the size of the previous record of KRW 20.3 trillion, set in 2020 and marks the largest ever by a Korean company. The record-breaking shareholder return is […]]]></description>
																<content:encoded><![CDATA[
<p>Samsung Electronics’ Board of Directors convened today and approved a shareholder return plan for 2026, estimated at approximately KRW 90 trillion to KRW 110 trillion.</p>



<p>This is approximately five times the size of the previous record of KRW 20.3 trillion, set in 2020 and marks the largest ever by a Korean company.</p>



<p>The record-breaking shareholder return is intended to ensure that the Company’s growth translates into tangible benefits for shareholders, fostering a virtuous cycle in which corporate growth and shareholder value creation reinforce each other.</p>



<p>In addition, Samsung is faithfully implementing its three-year shareholder return policy (2024–2026), which commits to allocating 50% of its free cash flow (FCF) to shareholder returns.</p>



<p>From 2024 to 2025, Samsung paid out a total of KRW 19.6 trillion in regular dividends, at KRW 9.8 trillion annually. In 2025, the Company also executed a special dividend of KRW 1.3 trillion and a share buyback and cancellation worth KRW 8.4 trillion.</p>



<p>Including the 2026 shareholder return, the total shareholder return for the three-year period (2024–2026) is expected to reach between KRW 120 trillion and KRW 140 trillion.</p>



<p>In the third quarter of 2026, Samsung plans to distribute approximately KRW 30 trillion in cash dividends, including regular dividends, with specific details to be finalized at the October Board meeting.</p>



<p>The remaining shareholder return will be determined at the January Board meeting in 2027, once the 2026 financial performance is confirmed. The plan will include cash dividends and share buybacks/ cancellations. The Board today also approved a share buyback worth about KRW15 trillion for employee compensation.</p>



<p>The share buyback for employee compensation is also expected to contribute to enhancing shareholder value.</p>



<p>Moving forward, the Samsung remains committed to faithfully implementing its shareholder return policy and actively communicating its plans with shareholders and the market.</p>
]]></content:encoded>

				<title><![CDATA[Samsung’s Human-Centered Design Wins IDEA and Red Dot Design Award 2026]]></title>
				<link>https://news.samsung.com/global/samsungs-human-centered-design-wins-idea-and-red-dot-design-award-2026</link>
				<pubDate>Fri, 21 Aug 2026 16:00:00 +0000</pubDate>
								<media:content url="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/08/21130927/Samsung-Corporate-Design-IDEA-2026-Red-Dot-Design-Award-2026-45-Honors-and-12-Awards_Thumb932_F-728x410.jpg" medium="image" />
				<dc:creator><![CDATA[Samsung Newsroom]]></dc:creator>

									<description><![CDATA[Samsung Electronics today announced that it has received significant honors at the International Design Excellence Awards (IDEA) 2026 and the Red Dot Design Award 2026. These accolades include one Impact Award, nine Bronze awards, and 35 Finalist recognitions at IDEA along with 12 awards in the Red Dot Design Award Brands & Communication Design category […]]]></description>
																<content:encoded><![CDATA[
<p>Samsung Electronics today announced that it has received significant honors at the International Design Excellence Awards (IDEA) 2026 and the Red Dot Design Award 2026. These accolades include one Impact Award, nine Bronze awards, and 35 Finalist recognitions at IDEA along with 12 awards in the Red Dot Design Award Brands & Communication Design category — including one Best of the Best award.</p>



<p>“Design has the power to connect ideas with people and turn them into meaningful experiences,” said Mauro Porcini, President and Chief Design Officer of the Device eXperience (DX) Division at Samsung Electronics. “At Samsung, we will continue to deepen our understanding of people, uncover new possibilities in everyday moments, and create experiences that enrich lives and shape a better future.”</p>



<p><a href="https://www.idsa.org/awards-recognitions/idea/" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer noopener">IDEA</a> is one of the world’s most prestigious design award programs and was founded by the Industrial Designers Society of America (IDSA) in 1980. This year, the organization selected winners across 20 categories, including Home, Consumer Technology, and Digital Interaction. Key evaluation criteria include Design Innovation, Benefit to User, Benefit to Society, and more.</p>



<p>Meanwhile, the Red Dot Design Award is widely recognized as one of the world’s premier design honors. Established in 1955 and organized by German organization Design Zentrum Nordrhein Westfalen, it recognizes excellence across three categories: Product Design, Design Concept, and Brands & Communication Design. Specifically, Red Dot Award: Brands & Communication Design recognizes and celebrates creative approaches that shape how brands communicate and connect with people.</p>



<p>Samsung was recognized by these organizations for expanding its design excellence beyond products to encompass services, brand experiences, and forward-looking design concepts.</p>



<p></p>



<h2 class="wp-block-heading"><strong>IDEA 2026: 45 Honors, Including One Impact Award</strong></h2>



<p>Samsung’s <strong>Accessibility Design for Home Appliances</strong> received the inaugural <strong>Impact Award </strong>at IDEA 2026. Introduced this year, the award is presented as an additional honor to select winning designs that demonstrate strong sustainability and social impact.</p>



<figure class="wp-block-image size-full"><img width="1000" height="563" src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/08/21102243/Samsung-Corporate-Design-IDEA-2026-Red-Dot-Design-Award-2026-45-Honors-and-12-Awards_main1_FF.jpg" alt="Samsung’s Accessibility Design for Home Appliances received the inaugural Impact Award at IDEA 2026." class="wp-image-176650" /></figure>



<p>Accessibility Design for Home Appliances considers the diverse needs of users with visual, hearing, mobility, and cognitive differences, helping everyone use home appliances like washers and refrigerators more easily and conveniently. Key features include <strong>Easy Mode</strong>, which simplifies complex functions and provides larger on-screen text and voice guidance, and <strong>Auto Open Door</strong>, which allows users with limited physical strength to open appliance doors with a gentle touch or voice command.</p>



<p>Accessibility settings configured on a mobile device can also be carried over to connected home appliances. <strong>Voice ID</strong> recognizes individual users and automatically applies their personalized settings, as well, allowing multiple users to enjoy a convenient appliance experience tailored to their individual needs and preferences.</p>



<p>Nine Samsung designs were honored with Bronze awards at IDEA 2026 for their innovation in premium appliances, branding, and sustainable design. These honorees include:</p>



<figure class="wp-block-image size-full"><img width="1000" height="563" src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/08/21101904/Samsung-Corporate-Design-IDEA-2026-Red-Dot-Design-Award-2026-45-Honors-and-12-Awards_main2.jpg" alt="Infinite AI Air Purifier" class="wp-image-176644" /></figure>



<ul class="wp-block-list">
<li><strong>Infinite AI Air Purifier:</strong> a premium air purifier featuring soft curved lines and a pop-up clean booster that elegantly integrates into any space.</li>
</ul>



<figure class="wp-block-image size-full"><img width="1000" height="563" src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/08/21101919/Samsung-Corporate-Design-IDEA-2026-Red-Dot-Design-Award-2026-45-Honors-and-12-Awards_main3.jpg" alt="Micro RGB" class="wp-image-176645" /></figure>



<ul class="wp-block-list">
<li><strong>Micro RGB Brand Identity:</strong> visually expresses the superior picture quality and immersive display experience of Micro RGB technology.</li>
</ul>



<figure class="wp-block-image size-full"><img loading="lazy" width="1000" height="667" src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/08/21101933/Samsung-Corporate-Design-IDEA-2026-Red-Dot-Design-Award-2026-45-Honors-and-12-Awards_main4.jpg" alt="Portable SSD T7 Resurrected" class="wp-image-176646" /></figure>



<ul class="wp-block-list">
<li><strong>Portable SSD T7 Resurrected:</strong> a premium portable storage device with an enclosure crafted entirely from recycled aluminum.</li>
</ul>



<figure class="wp-block-image size-full"><img loading="lazy" width="1000" height="563" src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/08/21101955/Samsung-Corporate-Design-IDEA-2026-Red-Dot-Design-Award-2026-45-Honors-and-12-Awards_main5.jpg" alt="Samsung Mobile Origami Package" class="wp-image-176647" /></figure>



<ul class="wp-block-list">
<li><strong>Samsung Mobile Origami Package:</strong> an advanced smartphone packaging concept that applies origami folding techniques using a single sheet of recycled paper.</li>
</ul>



<figure class="wp-block-image size-full"><img loading="lazy" width="1000" height="563" src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/08/21102012/Samsung-Corporate-Design-IDEA-2026-Red-Dot-Design-Award-2026-45-Honors-and-12-Awards_main6.jpg" alt="AI Design Companion (AIDC)" class="wp-image-176648" /></figure>



<ul class="wp-block-list">
<li><strong>AI Design Companion (AIDC):</strong> an AI-based advanced design solution that leverages AI, digital twins and virtual simulation to rapidly validate and verify product wearability performance and usability.</li>
</ul>



<p>Samsung also had 35 designs honored as IDEA 2026 Finalists.</p>



<p></p>



<h2 class="wp-block-heading"><strong>Red Dot Award 2026: 12 Awards, Including Best of the Best for Galaxy’s Privacy Display</strong></h2>



<p>Notable among Samsung’s 12 winning designs in the Brands & Communication Design category at the Red Dot Design Award 2026 was Galaxy Privacy Display, which won the Best of the Best award.</p>



<figure class="wp-block-image size-full"><img loading="lazy" width="1000" height="540" src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/08/21103017/Samsung-Corporate-Design-IDEA-2026-Red-Dot-Design-Award-2026-45-Honors-and-12-Awards_main7F.jpg" alt="Privacy Display on Galaxy S26 Ultra" class="wp-image-176655" /></figure>



<p>Introduced on Galaxy S26 Ultra as the mobile industry’s first built-in privacy-protecting display, Privacy Display is designed to protect personal information in everyday situations such as public transit, cafés, and shared environments. Users can customize when the feature activates — such as when entering PINs, patterns, and passwords, or opening selected apps — and adjust privacy levels depending on the situation. By allowing the screen to remain clearly visible from the front while limiting visibility from surrounding angles, Privacy Display offers a more seamless and intuitive way to protect privacy.</p>



<p>Samsung also won 11 awards in the Red Dot Award 2026 Brands & Communication Design category for innovations that enhance the user’s quality of life through more personalized experiences. Key award-winning projects include:</p>



<ul class="wp-block-list">
<li><strong>The Movingstyle Visual Branding:</strong> expresses the freedom and versatility of a portable screen through a distinctive visual identity.</li>
</ul>



<figure class="wp-block-image size-full"><img loading="lazy" width="1000" height="563" src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/08/21103236/Samsung-Corporate-Design-IDEA-2026-Red-Dot-Design-Award-2026-45-Honors-and-12-Awards_main8.jpg" alt="The Movingstyle" class="wp-image-176656" /></figure>



<ul class="wp-block-list">
<li><strong>Vision AI Companion: </strong>provides a personalized experience by combining real-time viewing context with user preferences.</li>
</ul>



<figure class="wp-block-image size-full"><img loading="lazy" width="1000" height="563" src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/08/21103305/Samsung-Corporate-Design-IDEA-2026-Red-Dot-Design-Award-2026-45-Honors-and-12-Awards_main9.jpg" alt="Vision AI Companion" class="wp-image-176657" /></figure>



<ul class="wp-block-list">
<li><strong>Design is an Act of Love</strong><strong>:</strong> an immersive exhibition at Milan Design Week 2026 that offered a glimpse into Samsung’s evolving design vision and explored how design can shape everyday life and new ways of living. </li>
</ul>



<figure class="wp-block-image size-full"><img loading="lazy" width="1000" height="563" src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/08/21103326/Samsung-Corporate-Design-IDEA-2026-Red-Dot-Design-Award-2026-45-Honors-and-12-Awards_main10.jpg" alt="Design is an Act of Love exhibition at Milan Design Week 2026" class="wp-image-176658" /></figure>



<ul class="wp-block-list">
<li><strong>Galaxy XR Package</strong><strong>:</strong> designed with an internal structure that allows it to be repurposed as a long-term storage and display solution.</li>
</ul>



<figure class="wp-block-image size-full"><img loading="lazy" width="1000" height="667" src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/08/21103341/Samsung-Corporate-Design-IDEA-2026-Red-Dot-Design-Award-2026-45-Honors-and-12-Awards_main11.jpg" alt="Galaxy XR package " class="wp-image-176659" /></figure>



<ul class="wp-block-list">
<li><a href="https://www.samsung.com/in/business/industries/biot/?srsltid=AfmBOoptzQDOHvfqJT1dLmH94twPYY15itkpfS7sp176W2Se9wW5R6TJ" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer noopener"><strong>Samsung b.IoT Digital Twin</strong></a><strong>: </strong>recreates real-world buildings as 3D models in a virtual environment and maximizes operational efficiency by visualizing the status of building systems in real time<strong>.</strong></li>
</ul>



<figure class="wp-block-image size-full"><img loading="lazy" width="1000" height="563" src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/08/21103354/Samsung-Corporate-Design-IDEA-2026-Red-Dot-Design-Award-2026-45-Honors-and-12-Awards_main12.jpg" alt="Samsung b.IoT Digital Twin" class="wp-image-176660" /></figure>

At Galaxy Unpacked July 2026 in London on July 22, Dr. Hon Pak, Senior Vice President and Head of the Digital Health Team, Mobile eXperience (MX) Business at Samsung Electronics, sat down with cognitive neuroscientist and science communicator Dr. Dong-seon Chang to […]]]></description>
																<content:encoded><![CDATA[
<p>Managing health can be complicated. But what if it didn’t have to be?</p>



<p>At Galaxy Unpacked July 2026 in London on July 22, Dr. Hon Pak, Senior Vice President and Head of the Digital Health Team, Mobile eXperience (MX) Business at Samsung Electronics, sat down with cognitive neuroscientist and science communicator Dr. Dong-seon Chang to explore how AI could make health management simpler and more connected.</p>



<p>Dr. Pak explained how the body changes from day to day and how AI can learn an individual’s daily rhythms and patterns to help transform fragmented health data into more meaningful, personalized guidance. He also introduced Samsung Health’s Connected Care vision, extending beyond devices and data to connect people — from healthcare professionals and families to partners across the broader health ecosystem.</p>



<p>Ultimately, the conversation pointed to a broader question of how society can embrace AI while keeping people at the center.</p>



<p>Watch the video below to learn more about Samsung’s vision for healthcare that is simpler, more connected, and human-centered.</p>

				<title><![CDATA[Samsung Introduces New Over-the-Range Microwave With DualVent Technology]]></title>
				<link>https://news.samsung.com/global/samsung-introduces-new-over-the-range-microwave-with-dualvent-technology</link>
				<pubDate>Thu, 20 Aug 2026 08:00:00 +0000</pubDate>
								<media:content url="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/08/19093430/Samsung-Digital-Appliances-New-Over-the-Range-Microwave-DualVent-Technology_Thumb932-728x410.jpg" medium="image" />
				<dc:creator><![CDATA[Samsung Newsroom]]></dc:creator>
						<category><![CDATA[Home Appliances]]></category>
		<category><![CDATA[Press Releases]]></category>
		<category><![CDATA[Auto Connectivity]]></category>
		<category><![CDATA[DualVent Technology]]></category>
		<category><![CDATA[Over-the-Range Microwave]]></category>
		<category><![CDATA[Ventilation system]]></category>
                <guid isPermaLink="false">https://bit.ly/4x3YoJ6</guid>
									<description><![CDATA[Samsung Electronics today announced its new Over-the-Range Microwave in the U.S. and Canada beginning September 2026. The new model introduces DualVent technology, a ventilation system with air intakes at both the bottom and front of the microwave. Additional features include Auto Connectivity and a fingerprint resistant finish that helps maintain the microwave’s clean appearance. “Consumers […]]]></description>
																<content:encoded><![CDATA[
<figure class="wp-block-image size-full"><img loading="lazy" width="1000" height="563" src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/08/18181348/Samsung-Digital-Appliances-New-Over-the-Range-Microwave-DualVent-Technology_Main1.jpg" alt="An image of ME80H30" class="wp-image-176609" /></figure>



<p>Samsung Electronics today announced its new Over-the-Range Microwave in the U.S. and Canada beginning September 2026. The new model introduces DualVent technology, a ventilation system with air intakes at both the bottom and front of the microwave. Additional features include Auto Connectivity and a fingerprint resistant finish that helps maintain the microwave’s clean appearance.</p>



<p>“Consumers value products that bring together stunning design and practical features that elevate the cooking experience in modern kitchens,” said Hyoung Min Park, VP and Head of Customer Experience Team of the Digital Appliances (DA) Business at Samsung Electronics. “The new Samsung Over-the-Range Microwave with DualVent technology brings powerful ventilation and convenient cooking features into one practical form factor, which saves space and makes a style statement.”</p>



<p></p>



<h2 class="wp-block-heading"><strong>Two Air Intakes To Help Capture Cooking Fumes</strong><sup data-fn="db39393c-177b-4853-ba58-30fda09129d3" class="fn"><a href="#db39393c-177b-4853-ba58-30fda09129d3" id="db39393c-177b-4853-ba58-30fda09129d3-link">1</a></sup></h2>



<p>DualVent technology uses two air intakes placed at the bottom and front of the microwave to help capture fumes from across the cooktop below. The front intake provides additional coverage for the front burners consumers use most, while the bottom intake helps capture fumes from the rest of the cooktop.</p>



<p>By capturing fumes from all burners, DualVent technology achieves a fume capture rate of up to 82%, delivering ventilation performance comparable to standalone hoods from Samsung under the same test conditions. This allows consumers to enjoy powerful ventilation while preserving the space-saving benefits of the form factor.</p>



<figure class="wp-block-image size-full"><img loading="lazy" width="1000" height="563" src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/08/18181536/Samsung-Digital-Appliances-New-Over-the-Range-Microwave-DualVent-Technology_Main2.jpg" alt="An image of DualVent Technology" class="wp-image-176610" /></figure>



<p></p>



<h2 class="wp-block-heading"><strong>Convenient Ventilation With Automatic Controls</strong></h2>



<p>Extra vents often need to be pushed or pulled to be used, but that is not the case with Samsung’s DualVent, which is activated with just a single tap on the LED-lit controls menu. When tapped, the newly designed front blades open seamlessly to support powerful dual-intake performance. Then, the blades close automatically when ventilation is turned off, with no manual flipping or pushing required.</p>



<p>The cooking experience is further simplified by Auto Connectivity, which wirelessly aligns the status of the microwave<sup data-fn="ee513617-8687-4de9-a732-df07fd4a7723" class="fn"><a href="#ee513617-8687-4de9-a732-df07fd4a7723" id="ee513617-8687-4de9-a732-df07fd4a7723-link">2</a></sup> with compatible Samsung cooktops or range ovens. With it, users can use the one-touch controls to control both the cooktop and the Over-the-Range Microwave, turning both appliances on and off simultaneously.</p>



<hr class="wp-block-separator has-alpha-channel-opacity" />


<ol class="wp-block-footnotes"><li id="db39393c-177b-4853-ba58-30fda09129d3">82% capture rate from chamber tests conducted at ICE&KS Pre-Development Lab on Feb. 9, 2026 using CO2 tracer method per LBNL (2020), evaluating two intakes and one exhaust vs. standalone hoods under identical conditions. Actual results may vary depending on individual use and circumstances. DualVent requires installation per guidelines. Performance varies with installation, duct type, cooking method, and environment. <a href="#db39393c-177b-4853-ba58-30fda09129d3-link" aria-label="Jump to footnote reference 1"><img src="https://s.w.org/images/core/emoji/16.0.1/72x72/21a9.png" alt="↩" class="wp-smiley" style="height: 1em; max-height: 1em;" />︎</a></li><li id="ee513617-8687-4de9-a732-df07fd4a7723">Only available when used in combination with certain Samsung cooktops or range ovens that have Wi-Fi connectivity. Each appliance should be connected to a Wi-Fi network and registered in the Samsung SmartThings App. Ventilation fan will automatically operate for safety if the temperature at the bottom of the product is high. This function takes precedence over Auto Connectivity. <a href="#ee513617-8687-4de9-a732-df07fd4a7723-link" aria-label="Jump to footnote reference 2"><img src="https://s.w.org/images/core/emoji/16.0.1/72x72/21a9.png" alt="↩" class="wp-smiley" style="height: 1em; max-height: 1em;" />︎</a></li></ol>]]></content:encoded>

					<item>
				<title><![CDATA[[Video] Samsung AI Assistant Helps Teachers Create More Engaging and Accessible Lessons]]></title>
				<link>https://news.samsung.com/global/video-samsung-ai-assistant-helps-teachers-create-more-engaging-and-accessible-lessons</link>
				<pubDate>Wed, 19 Aug 2026 11:00:00 +0000</pubDate>
								<media:content url="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/08/18164518/Samsung-TVs-and-Displays-Samsung-AI-Assistant-Classroom_Thumb932-728x410.jpg" medium="image" />
				<dc:creator><![CDATA[Samsung Newsroom]]></dc:creator>
						<category><![CDATA[TVs & Displays]]></category>
		<category><![CDATA[Interactive Display]]></category>
		<category><![CDATA[Samsung AI Assistant]]></category>
		<category><![CDATA[Samsung Education Portal]]></category>
		<category><![CDATA[VIDEO NEWS]]></category>
                <guid isPermaLink="false">https://bit.ly/45zhoTu</guid>
									<description><![CDATA[Helping every student stay engaged and supported throughout a lesson requires teachers to balance instruction, interaction, and individual learning needs. Managing these different needs while maintaining lesson flow can add to a teacher’s workload. Samsung AI Assistant is a built-in app for compatible Android-based Samsung Interactive Displays that helps teachers support every stage of a […]]]></description>
																<content:encoded><![CDATA[
<p>Helping every student stay engaged and supported throughout a lesson requires teachers to balance instruction, interaction, and individual learning needs. Managing these different needs while maintaining lesson flow can add to a teacher’s workload.</p>



<p>Samsung AI Assistant is a built-in app for compatible Android-based Samsung Interactive Displays<sup data-fn="2c9fe1ac-84bb-4134-8ed3-106fb47fa7a5" class="fn"><a href="#2c9fe1ac-84bb-4134-8ed3-106fb47fa7a5" id="2c9fe1ac-84bb-4134-8ed3-106fb47fa7a5-link">1</a></sup> that helps teachers support every stage of a lesson with AI-powered features.<sup data-fn="28a70abb-3c60-402a-b73a-77289fe08e1f" class="fn"><a href="#28a70abb-3c60-402a-b73a-77289fe08e1f" id="28a70abb-3c60-402a-b73a-77289fe08e1f-link">2</a></sup> With features including Live Transcript, Circle to Search, AI Quiz and My Lessons, teachers can support more accessible instructions, quickly bring additional learning resources into class and review student understanding.</p>



<p>Watch the video below to see how Samsung AI Assistant helps teachers enhance classroom engagement across every stage of a lesson.</p>



<figure class="wp-block-embed is-type-video is-provider-youtube wp-block-embed-youtube wp-embed-aspect-16-9 wp-has-aspect-ratio"><div class="wp-block-embed__wrapper">
<iframe loading="lazy" title="Interactive Display: Make Every Lesson More Engaging with AI Assistant | Samsung" width="500" height="281" src="https://www.youtube.com/embed/vl9r5mTBaiA?feature=oembed" frameborder="0" allow="accelerometer; autoplay; clipboard-write; encrypted-media; gyroscope; picture-in-picture; web-share" referrerpolicy="strict-origin-when-cross-origin" allowfullscreen></iframe>
</div></figure>



<p></p>



<h2 class="wp-block-heading"><strong>Live Transcript: Begin Class With More Accessible Instruction</strong></h2>



<p>In classrooms, it is important that every student can understand and follow classroom instruction. In larger lecture rooms, the teacher’s voice can be harder to hear, and for some students, spoken instruction alone may not always be enough.</p>



<figure class="wp-block-image size-full"><img loading="lazy" width="1000" height="563" src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/08/18144734/Samsung-TVs-and-Displays-Samsung-AI-Assistant-Classroom_main1.gif" alt="After turning on Live Transcript and starting Voice Recording, teachers’ spoken instructions are converted into real-time text and displayed on the Samsung Interactive Display." class="wp-image-176559" /><figcaption class="wp-element-caption">▲ After turning on Live Transcript and starting Voice Recording, teachers’ spoken instructions are converted into real-time text and displayed on the Samsung Interactive Display.</figcaption></figure>



<p>Samsung AI Assistant’s Live Transcript<sup data-fn="7cad555c-b707-41b0-ad67-f1fc95995446" class="fn"><a href="#7cad555c-b707-41b0-ad67-f1fc95995446" id="7cad555c-b707-41b0-ad67-f1fc95995446-link">3</a></sup> feature converts teachers’ spoken instructions into real-time text displayed on the screen, helping students follow along with the lesson. Teachers can adjust the transcript’s language, font size and screen position to accommodate different classroom needs.</p>



<p>When paired with Samsung’s screen sharing app,<sup data-fn="b5ea71af-5e1c-45c0-bd84-0dae482d5ed7" class="fn"><a href="#b5ea71af-5e1c-45c0-bd84-0dae482d5ed7" id="b5ea71af-5e1c-45c0-bd84-0dae482d5ed7-link">4</a></sup> students can also view the Live Transcript on their own devices,<sup data-fn="36c3852a-f848-40f6-afba-6f08effeb358" class="fn"><a href="#36c3852a-f848-40f6-afba-6f08effeb358" id="36c3852a-f848-40f6-afba-6f08effeb358-link">5</a></sup> helping them stay connected to the lesson even if they are seated farther from the display or have difficulty hearing.</p>



<figure class="wp-block-image size-full"><img loading="lazy" width="1000" height="563" src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/08/18144811/Samsung-TVs-and-Displays-Samsung-AI-Assistant-Classroom_main2.jpg" alt="Students can view the Live Transcript on their personal devices using Samsung’s screen sharing app." class="wp-image-176560" /><figcaption class="wp-element-caption">▲ Students can view the Live Transcript on their personal devices using Samsung’s screen sharing app.</figcaption></figure>



<p>Grant Wilson, Solution Architect AV/IFP at STS Education, highlighted how Live Transcript can help students stay connected and better follow classroom instruction.</p>



<p>“Kids these days are turning on transcripts, even as they’re watching movies at home, and it’s helping with their understanding,” said Wilson. “What I really like is that you can pair it with the Samsung screen sharing app, so every student in the classroom has a front-row seat to the screen from their own device.”</p>



<p></p>



<h2 class="wp-block-heading"><strong>Circle to Search: Expand Context in Every Lesson</strong></h2>



<p>During the lesson, teachers may need to introduce additional examples and resources to help students connect classroom concepts to real-world context. Finding relevant materials during a lesson, however, can take time.</p>



<p>Circle to Search lets teachers circle any image or object displayed on the screen to quickly find related images, videos and relevant web resources.<sup data-fn="f1e4b3a2-af74-4a0e-a361-2113d6bca1b6" class="fn"><a href="#f1e4b3a2-af74-4a0e-a361-2113d6bca1b6" id="f1e4b3a2-af74-4a0e-a361-2113d6bca1b6-link">6</a></sup> Teachers can add search results directly to the whiteboard by pressing and holding the selected results, making it easier to introduce diverse examples and expand on a topic while keeping students focused.</p>



<figure class="wp-block-image size-full"><img loading="lazy" width="1000" height="280" src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/08/18145007/Samsung-TVs-and-Displays-Samsung-AI-Assistant-Classroom_main3.jpg" alt="Circle to Search helps teachers quickly find additional learning resources such as related images, videos and web links without leaving the lesson." class="wp-image-176561" /><figcaption class="wp-element-caption">▲ Circle to Search helps teachers quickly find additional learning resources such as related images, videos and web links without leaving the lesson.</figcaption></figure>



<p>Tambra Clark, Technology Integration Facilitator at Birmingham City Schools and a Samsung Solve for Tomorrow Top 10 winner, highlighted how Circle to Search helps teachers quickly bring relevant resources into lessons.</p>



<p>“Being able to use that Circle to Search feature just saves a lot of time where you don’t have to go in and type in certain words, content or videos,” said Clark. “Everything comes up at once simply by circling that word.”</p>



<figure class="wp-block-image size-full"><img loading="lazy" width="1000" height="563" src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/08/18145044/Samsung-TVs-and-Displays-Samsung-AI-Assistant-Classroom_main4.jpg" alt="Tambra Clark, Technology Integration Facilitator at Birmingham City Schools" class="wp-image-176562" /><figcaption class="wp-element-caption">▲ Tambra Clark, Technology Integration Facilitator at Birmingham City Schools</figcaption></figure>



<p></p>



<h2 class="wp-block-heading"><strong>AI Quiz and My Lessons: Review Learning and Continue Beyond the Classroom</strong></h2>



<p>As the lesson wraps up, Samsung AI Assistant helps teachers review student understanding using AI Quiz.</p>



<p>Teachers can generate quizzes based on transcripts from recorded lessons and uploaded materials. Because AI Quiz generates questions based on lesson transcripts and uploaded materials, it helps keep quizzes aligned with what was taught in class.</p>



<figure class="wp-block-image size-full"><img loading="lazy" width="1000" height="563" src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/08/18145127/Samsung-TVs-and-Displays-Samsung-AI-Assistant-Classroom_main5.jpg" alt="Teachers can create an AI Quiz by selecting options such as quiz type (multiple choice or true/false), number of questions, time per question and difficulty level." class="wp-image-176563" /><figcaption class="wp-element-caption">▲ Teachers can create an AI Quiz by selecting options such as quiz type (multiple choice or true/false), number of questions, time per question and difficulty level.</figcaption></figure>



<p>After a teacher creates an AI Quiz, students can join the quiz from their own devices using a QR code or link. Once students submit their answers, teachers can reveal the correct answers on the screen after each question and provide explanations. The results dashboard then helps teachers review comprehension and identify topics that may require further instruction.</p>



<p>“You can see where the students are at, and you’re able to identify areas of improvement with that quiz,” said Clark.</p>



<figure class="wp-block-image size-full"><img loading="lazy" width="1000" height="321" src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/08/18145200/Samsung-TVs-and-Displays-Samsung-AI-Assistant-Classroom_main6.jpg" alt="(Left) AI Quiz lets students respond to quiz questions and displays the correct answers for teachers to explain. (Right) After the AI Quiz session, teachers can review results through the dashboard, including completion rate, average score and correct answer rate for each question." class="wp-image-176564" /><figcaption class="wp-element-caption">▲ (Left) AI Quiz lets students respond to quiz questions and displays the correct answers for teachers to explain. (Right) After the AI Quiz session, teachers can review results through the dashboard, including completion rate, average score and correct answer rate for each question.</figcaption></figure>



<p>Beyond quizzes, voice-recorded lessons are automatically saved in My Lessons, where teachers can revisit transcripts, review AI-generated summaries and share materials for future classes.</p>



<p>Shaun Taylor, Director of Sales at STS Education, highlighted how these features can help teachers adapt instruction for every student in the classroom.</p>



<p>“The word that I would use from my experience is differentiation,” said Taylor. “Having tools that allow students to engage at their own pace helps support different learning needs.”</p>



<figure class="wp-block-image size-full"><img loading="lazy" width="1000" height="562" src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/08/18145249/Samsung-TVs-and-Displays-Samsung-AI-Assistant-Classroom_main7.jpg" alt="(Left) Grant Wilson, Solution Architect AV/IFP at STS Education (Right) Shaun Taylor, Director of Sales at STS Education" class="wp-image-176565" /><figcaption class="wp-element-caption">▲ (Left) Grant Wilson, Solution Architect AV/IFP at STS Education (Right) Shaun Taylor, Director of Sales at STS Education</figcaption></figure>



<p>From supporting accessible instruction to expanding classroom exploration and helping teachers review student understanding, Samsung AI Assistant enables more engaging and accessible learning experiences directly through Samsung Interactive Displays.</p>



<hr class="wp-block-separator has-alpha-channel-opacity" />

				<title><![CDATA[Samsung Electronics To Establish HVAC Production Line in Korea]]></title>
				<link>https://news.samsung.com/global/samsung-electronics-to-establish-hvac-production-line-in-korea</link>
				<pubDate>Wed, 19 Aug 2026 09:30:00 +0000</pubDate>
								<media:content url="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/08/18141530/Samsung-Digital-Appliances-HAVC-Production-Line_Thumb932-728x410.jpg" medium="image" />
				<dc:creator><![CDATA[Samsung Newsroom]]></dc:creator>
						<category><![CDATA[Home Appliances]]></category>
		<category><![CDATA[Press Releases]]></category>
		<category><![CDATA[Ai data centers]]></category>
		<category><![CDATA[FläktGroup]]></category>
		<category><![CDATA[HVAC]]></category>
                <guid isPermaLink="false">https://bit.ly/4wDYtSV</guid>
									<description><![CDATA[Samsung Electronics today announced that it will establish a FläktGroup HVAC production line in Korea to target the rapidly growing HVAC market, including the production of advanced cooling solutions for AI data centers. The company plans to invest approximately KRW 240 billion (USD 158 million) to build a 21,500m2 HVAC production line at its operations […]]]></description>
																<content:encoded><![CDATA[
<figure class="wp-block-image size-full"><img loading="lazy" width="1000" height="684" src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/08/18141700/Samsung-Digital-Appliances-HAVC-Production-Line_Main1.jpg" alt="Samsung Electronics(Gwangju)" class="wp-image-176546" /></figure>



<p>Samsung Electronics today announced that it will establish a FläktGroup HVAC production line in Korea to target the rapidly growing HVAC market, including the production of advanced cooling solutions for AI data centers.</p>



<p>The company plans to invest approximately KRW 240 billion (USD 158 million) to build a 21,500m<sup>2</sup> HVAC production line at its operations site in Gwangju, Korea. The new production line is scheduled to begin operations upon completion by 2028. It will manufacture HVAC products from FläktGroup, which was acquired by Samsung Electronics <a href="https://news.samsung.com/global/samsung-electronics-acquires-leading-global-hvac-solutions-provider-flaktgroup" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer noopener">last year.</a></p>



<p>“Samsung’s acquisition of FläktGroup last year puts us in a unique position to expand the HVAC business and transform it into a new growth engine, to become a global top-tier company by 2030,” said Cheolgi Kim, Executive Vice President and Head of Digital Appliances (DA) Business at Samsung Electronics. “This investment in the new production line in Korea will serve as a solid foundation for achieving this goal.”</p>



<p>FläktGroup recently invested in expanding its manufacturing footprint – which will operate soon – through Project GAGAN, a new production facility in Pune, Maharashtra, India. With a global network of 14 manufacturing facilities, the company actively supplies products across key markets including the U.S., Europe and India.</p>



<p>Together with FläktGroup, Samsung will leverage the establishment of the Gwangju production line, its 15th manufacturing facility worldwide, as a strategic springboard for a full-scale expansion into the Korean market.</p>



<p>Samsung plans to supply HVAC facilities and infrastructure across various industrial sectors in Korea, with a particular emphasis on the surge in demand for AI data centers. To achieve this, the company will produce advanced HVAC products, including the production of advanced cooling solutions for AI data centers.</p>



<p>David Dorney, CEO of FläktGroup, said, “By combining Samsung’s AI expertise with FläktGroup’s HVAC product capabilities, we will be positioned to become an HVAC partner of choice in Korea and across Asia.”</p>



<figure class="wp-block-image size-full"><img loading="lazy" width="1000" height="563" src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/08/18142459/Samsung-Digital-Appliances-HAVC-Production-Line_Main2.jpg" alt="Planned site for FläktGroup’s HVAC Production Line" class="wp-image-176547" /></figure>



<figure class="wp-block-image size-full"><img loading="lazy" width="1000" height="788" src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/08/18142511/Samsung-Digital-Appliances-HAVC-Production-Line_Main3.jpg" alt="HVAC (CDU 1)" class="wp-image-176548" /></figure>



<figure class="wp-block-image size-full"><img loading="lazy" width="1000" height="480" src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/08/18142523/Samsung-Digital-Appliances-HAVC-Production-Line_Main4.jpg" alt="HVAC (CDU 2)" class="wp-image-176549" /></figure>
]]></content:encoded>

																<content:encoded><![CDATA[
<p>When summer temperatures soar, there’s nothing quite like a refreshing drink or chilled treat to cool down. From iced coffee and fizzy sodas to chilled smoothies and sorbets, ice is an essential ingredient in summer favorites.</p>



<p>But different foods and drinks call for different types of ice. Small, crushed ice is ideal for quickly chilling a beverage, while larger, slower-melting ice helps keep it cold for longer. For larger ice such as sphere ice, clarity is another key characteristic.</p>



<p>Across its global Bespoke AI Refrigerator lineup, Samsung Electronics offers six types of ice designed with different drinking cultures and preferences in mind. Available ice types vary by region and model. Select models also feature technology that precisely controls the freezing rate to create clear, solid sphere ice. Check out the infographics below to learn more about Samsung’s carefully engineered ice-making technology, thoughtfully engineered down to the shape and transparency of the ice.</p>



<figure class="wp-block-image size-full"><img loading="lazy" width="1000" height="1000" src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/08/14174214/Samsung-Digital-Appliances-Bespoke-AI-Refrigerators-Ice-Making-Technology-Infographic_main1.gif" alt="Across its global Bespoke AI Refrigerator lineup, Samsung Electronics offers six types of ice." class="wp-image-176515" /></figure>

<figure class="wp-block-image size-full"><img loading="lazy" width="1000" height="1000" src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/08/14174234/Samsung-Digital-Appliances-Bespoke-AI-Refrigerators-Ice-Making-Technology-Infographic_main3.jpg" alt="Ice size" class="wp-image-176517" /></figure>



<figure class="wp-block-image size-full"><img loading="lazy" width="1000" height="1000" src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/08/14174245/Samsung-Digital-Appliances-Bespoke-AI-Refrigerators-Ice-Making-Technology-Infographic_main4.jpg" alt="Cubed, Crushed, Sphere, and Ice Bites" class="wp-image-176518" /></figure>

				<title><![CDATA[‘Warranty and care’ Menu Simplifies Device Care for New Foldables]]></title>
				<link>https://news.samsung.com/global/warranty-and-care-menu-simplifies-device-care-for-new-foldables</link>
				<pubDate>Tue, 18 Aug 2026 08:00:00 +0000</pubDate>
								<media:content url="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/08/14154108/Samsung-Mobile-Warranty-and-Care-Samsung-Care_Thumb932-728x410.jpg" medium="image" />
				<dc:creator><![CDATA[Samsung Newsroom]]></dc:creator>
						<category><![CDATA[Mobile]]></category>
		<category><![CDATA[Galaxy Z Flip8]]></category>
		<category><![CDATA[Galaxy Z Fold8]]></category>
		<category><![CDATA[Galaxy Z Fold8 Ultra]]></category>
		<category><![CDATA[One UI 9]]></category>
		<category><![CDATA[Samsung Care+]]></category>
		<category><![CDATA[Warranty and care]]></category>
                <guid isPermaLink="false">https://bit.ly/4hyZ3NG</guid>
									<description><![CDATA[Samsung users may have noticed something new on their Galaxy Z foldables — the Warranty and care menu. Managing warranties and coverage for a Galaxy mobile phone, Galaxy Watch, Galaxy Buds, and Galaxy Tab can be a hassle. That’s where Warranty and care comes in. Introduced with One UI 9, it brings essential device management […]]]></description>
																<content:encoded><![CDATA[
<p>Samsung users may have noticed something new on their <a href="https://news.samsung.com/global/samsung-galaxy-z-fold8-ultra-fold8-and-flip8foldables-perfected-for-every-way-of-living" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer noopener">Galaxy Z foldables</a> — the <strong>Warranty and care</strong><sup data-fn="5a7d6cfe-cd00-43cf-a6ab-7e75fd5e6cb4" class="fn"><a href="#5a7d6cfe-cd00-43cf-a6ab-7e75fd5e6cb4" id="5a7d6cfe-cd00-43cf-a6ab-7e75fd5e6cb4-link">1</a></sup> menu.</p>



<p>Managing warranties and coverage for a Galaxy mobile phone, Galaxy Watch, Galaxy Buds, and Galaxy Tab can be a hassle. That’s where Warranty and care comes in. Introduced with <strong>One UI 9</strong>, it brings essential device management features together in one place, letting users check warranty status, run diagnostics, book repairs, and get support in just a few taps — all without leaving Settings.</p>



<p>Here’s a closer look at what Warranty and care, available with One UI 9 on the Galaxy Z Fold8 Ultra, Galaxy Z Fold8, and Galaxy Z Flip8, offers.</p>



<p></p>



<h2 class="wp-block-heading"><strong>Check Warranty Status Across Galaxy Devices</strong></h2>



<p>Keeping track of warranty periods and expiration dates across Galaxy devices isn’t always easy. Warranty and care consolidates this information in a single hub.</p>



<figure class="wp-block-image size-full"><img loading="lazy" width="1000" height="699" src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/08/14171312/Samsung-Mobile-Warranty-and-Care-Samsung-Care_main1.jpg" alt="Warranty and care consolidates this information in a single hub." class="wp-image-176504" /></figure>



<figure class="wp-block-image size-full"><img loading="lazy" width="1000" height="699" src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/08/14171342/Samsung-Mobile-Warranty-and-Care-Samsung-Care_main2.jpg" alt="Access Warranty and care instantly from Settings." class="wp-image-176505" /><figcaption class="wp-element-caption">▲ Access Warranty and care instantly from Settings.</figcaption></figure>



<p>Available in Settings, Warranty and care displays information<sup data-fn="1c040dc1-8d25-426e-9599-0b77894733a1" class="fn"><a href="#1c040dc1-8d25-426e-9599-0b77894733a1" id="1c040dc1-8d25-426e-9599-0b77894733a1-link">2</a></sup> about the device in use at the top of the screen — including remaining warranty period and eligibility for <a href="https://www.samsung.com/sec/samsung-care-plus/im/" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer noopener"><strong>Samsung Care+</strong></a><strong> </strong>enrollment.<sup data-fn="72a9207a-0daa-4156-84e6-71cb0ac0614a" class="fn"><a href="#72a9207a-0daa-4156-84e6-71cb0ac0614a" id="72a9207a-0daa-4156-84e6-71cb0ac0614a-link">3</a></sup> Below, users can find the same details for other Galaxy devices linked to their Samsung account, making it easy to check warranty and coverage status across their Galaxy ecosystem.</p>



<p>For added protection beyond the standard warranty, users can easily explore and enroll in a Samsung Care+ plan that matches their needs.</p>



<p></p>



<h2 class="wp-block-heading"><strong>Resolve Device Issues With Diagnostics and Bixby</strong></h2>



<p>When something doesn’t feel quite right, quickly finding out what’s wrong can make all the difference. The built-in <strong>Diagnostics</strong> feature checks the device — including its battery, charging, sensors, touch response, and connectivity — to help pinpoint the issue and guide users to the next step, whether that’s a suggested solution or a repair request.</p>



<figure class="wp-block-image size-full"><img loading="lazy" width="1000" height="699" src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/08/14171559/Samsung-Mobile-Warranty-and-Care-Samsung-Care_main3.jpg" alt="Bixby offers customized responses to your inquiries." class="wp-image-176506" /><figcaption class="wp-element-caption">▲ Bixby offers customized responses to your inquiries.</figcaption></figure>



<p>Users can also access <strong>Bixby</strong><sup data-fn="29ae218e-01b6-439f-a48b-2dd3114788a2" class="fn"><a href="#29ae218e-01b6-439f-a48b-2dd3114788a2" id="29ae218e-01b6-439f-a48b-2dd3114788a2-link">4</a></sup> from Warranty and care to ask about an issue and get help on the spot. For example, if a user asks, “My battery is draining too fast. What should I do?” Bixby can walk them through possible solutions step by step. It’s a convenient way to get answers to everyday questions without switching screens.</p>



<p></p>



<h2 class="wp-block-heading"><strong>Book Repairs and Check Estimated Costs in One Place</strong></h2>



<p>When professional help is needed, users can tap the “Request a repair” to book a repair at a <strong>Samsung-authorized service center</strong> without having to make phone calls. All repairs are carried out by Samsung-certified technicians using genuine parts. If users are unable to visit a service center, they can start remote support from the same screen and get help directly on their device.</p>



<p>Warranty and care also makes device care service information easy to check. After selecting an issue, users can view estimated repair costs<sup data-fn="768ae12d-0d07-4065-afd6-afd8b9dcf7d6" class="fn"><a href="#768ae12d-0d07-4065-afd6-afd8b9dcf7d6" id="768ae12d-0d07-4065-afd6-afd8b9dcf7d6-link">5</a></sup> for major parts alongside the cost of the same repair with Samsung Care+. This comparison helps users weigh their options and decide whether additional protection is worthwhile.</p>



<figure class="wp-block-image size-full"><img loading="lazy" width="1000" height="699" src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/08/14171637/Samsung-Mobile-Warranty-and-Care-Samsung-Care_main4.jpg" alt="Warranty and care also makes device care service information easy to check." class="wp-image-176507" /></figure>



<figure class="wp-block-image size-full"><img loading="lazy" width="1000" height="699" src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/08/14171651/Samsung-Mobile-Warranty-and-Care-Samsung-Care_main5.jpg" alt="Repair estimates on main parts can also be verified." class="wp-image-176508" /><figcaption class="wp-element-caption">▲ Repair estimates on main parts can also be verified.</figcaption></figure>



<h2 class="wp-block-heading"><strong>Protect Device Value for the Long Term</strong></h2>



<p>By bringing warranty information, diagnostics, support, and repair services together, Warranty and care gives Galaxy users a simpler way to manage their devices for greater peace of mind. Checking the feature during setup makes it easier to know where to turn if an issue comes up later — from running diagnostics and asking Bixby or a chatbot for help to requesting a repair. With these essential services close at hand, users can keep their devices in good condition for longer and help preserve their value.</p>

				<title><![CDATA[Samsung Partners With Kunsthistorisches Museum in Vienna To Bring Austrian Masterpieces to Samsung Art Store]]></title>
				<link>https://news.samsung.com/global/samsung-partners-with-kunsthistorisches-museum-in-vienna-to-bring-austrian-masterpieces-to-samsung-art-store</link>
				<pubDate>Tue, 18 Aug 2026 08:00:00 +0000</pubDate>
								<media:content url="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/08/13173531/Samsung-TVs-and-Displays-Samsung-Art-Store-Kunsthistorisches-Museum_Thumb932-728x410.jpg" medium="image" />
				<dc:creator><![CDATA[Samsung Newsroom]]></dc:creator>
						<category><![CDATA[Press Releases]]></category>
		<category><![CDATA[TVs & Displays]]></category>
		<category><![CDATA[Samsung Art Store]]></category>
		<category><![CDATA[Samsung TV]]></category>
                <guid isPermaLink="false">https://bit.ly/3Uw53Nq</guid>
									<description><![CDATA[Samsung Electronics and the Kunsthistorisches Museum in Vienna today announced a new partnership to bring 43 carefully selected masterpieces from the museum’s collection to Samsung Art Store. Starting today, art enthusiasts worldwide will be able to access these iconic works in stunning 4K resolution exclusively on compatible Samsung TVs. “The Kunsthistorisches Museum is not merely […]]]></description>
																<content:encoded><![CDATA[
<figure class="wp-block-image size-full"><img loading="lazy" width="1000" height="667" src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/08/13172859/Samsung-TVs-and-Displays-Samsung-Art-Store-Kunsthistorisches-Museum_main1.jpg" alt="(From left) “Vienna Viewed from the Belvedere Palace” (1758/61) by Bernardo Bellotto the Elder on OLED TV, “Summer” (1563) by Giuseppe Arcimboldo on The Frame and “The Tower of Babel” (1563) by Pieter Bruegel the Elder on Micro RGB TV at Kunsthistorisches Museum in Vienna. " class="wp-image-176470" /><figcaption class="wp-element-caption">▲ (From left) “Vienna Viewed from the Belvedere Palace” (1758/61) by Bernardo Bellotto the Elder on OLED TV, “Summer” (1563) by Giuseppe Arcimboldo on The Frame and “The Tower of Babel” (1563) by Pieter Bruegel the Elder on Micro RGB TV at Kunsthistorisches Museum in Vienna. Photo: Seniad Kasupovic</figcaption></figure>



<p>Samsung Electronics and the Kunsthistorisches Museum in Vienna today announced a new partnership to bring 43 carefully selected masterpieces from the museum’s collection to Samsung Art Store.<sup data-fn="240b6226-bc1c-48a1-bba5-33906bb554c3" class="fn"><a href="#240b6226-bc1c-48a1-bba5-33906bb554c3" id="240b6226-bc1c-48a1-bba5-33906bb554c3-link">1</a></sup> Starting today, art enthusiasts worldwide will be able to access these iconic works in stunning 4K resolution exclusively on compatible Samsung TVs.<sup data-fn="ea4b573e-6b99-4fdd-991e-8a3272e7b907" class="fn"><a href="#ea4b573e-6b99-4fdd-991e-8a3272e7b907" id="ea4b573e-6b99-4fdd-991e-8a3272e7b907-link">2</a></sup></p>



<p>“The Kunsthistorisches Museum is not merely an institution — it’s a symbol of Austrian cultural identity and is our neighbor, our partner and our home,” said Dario Casari, President of Samsung Austria and Samsung Switzerland. “This partnership represents something truly special for Samsung Austria — by combining these world-class cultural treasures with Samsung’s cutting-edge display technology, we’re creating a unique bridge between history and innovation, bringing Vienna’s artistic legacy to audiences around the globe.”</p>



<figure class="wp-block-image size-full"><img loading="lazy" width="1000" height="667" src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/08/13173113/Samsung-TVs-and-Displays-Samsung-Art-Store-Kunsthistorisches-Museum_main2.jpg" alt="(From left) “Vienna Viewed from the Belvedere Palace”, known as “Canaletto View” (1758/61) by Bernardo Bellotto on OLED TV and “The Art of Painting” (1665-68) by Johannes Vermeer on Micro RGB TV at Kunsthistorisches Museum. " class="wp-image-176471" /><figcaption class="wp-element-caption">▲ (From left) “Vienna Viewed from the Belvedere Palace”, known as “Canaletto View” (1758/61) by Bernardo Bellotto on OLED TV and “The Art of Painting” (1665-68) by Johannes Vermeer on Micro RGB TV at Kunsthistorisches Museum. Photo: Seniad Kasupovic</figcaption></figure>



<p></p>



<h2 class="wp-block-heading"><strong>Masterpieces from Bruegel to Vermeer Now Available Digitally</strong></h2>



<p>The Samsung Art Store collection features some of the most significant works from the Kunsthistorisches Museum’s painting gallery, including:</p>



<ul class="wp-block-list">
<li>Several paintings by Pieter Bruegel the Elder, including the famous “The Tower of Babel”</li>



<li>Johannes Vermeer’s “The Art of Painting”</li>



<li>Gustav Klimt’s “Nuda Veritas”</li>



<li>Canaletto and Bellotto’s iconic views of Venice and Vienna</li>



<li>Giuseppe Arcimboldo’s “Summer”</li>



<li>Works by Rubens, Velázquez, Caravaggio, Titian, Rembrandt, and Raphael</li>
</ul>



<p>Thanks to high-resolution digital rendering, viewers can appreciate the finest details of these extraordinary works. Bruegel’s “The Tower of Babel” reveals countless architectural and technical details, while Vermeer’s “The Art of Painting” impressively showcases the artist’s distinctive use of light and precision.</p>



<figure class="wp-block-image size-full"><img loading="lazy" width="1000" height="583" src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/08/13173210/Samsung-TVs-and-Displays-Samsung-Art-Store-Kunsthistorisches-Museum_main3.jpg" alt="" class="wp-image-176472" /><figcaption class="wp-element-caption">▲ “The Tower of Babel” (1563) by Pieter Bruegel the Elder on The Frame Pro.</figcaption></figure>



<p></p>



<h2 class="wp-block-heading"><strong>Bringing World-Class Cultural Heritage Directly to the Home</strong></h2>



<p>With the Kunsthistorisches Museum joining the Samsung Art Store platform, Samsung continues to expand its premium portfolio of international cultural institutions. Existing collaborations with renowned museums such as the Louvre in Paris, the Prado Museum in Madrid, and the MUNCH Museum in Oslo already offer users access to unique art collections directly on their Samsung TVs.</p>



<p>Samsung Art Store offers a curated selection of thousands of international artworks displayed in native resolution with precise color calibration. Users can personalize their viewing experience by selecting artworks according to style, period or mood, transforming their living spaces into their own digital gallery.</p>



<p>“We are delighted to join Samsung Art Store and share our unparalleled collection with a global audience,” said Dr. Jonathan Fine, General Director of KHM-Museumsverband. “By making these masterpieces accessible to everyone, regardless of location, we can engage a wider public. This digital experience will fascinate viewers and inspire them to encounter the original artworks in person at our museum in Vienna, bridging the gap between digital accessibility and the irreplaceable experience of standing before the originals.”</p>



<figure class="wp-block-image size-full"><img loading="lazy" width="1000" height="667" src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/08/13173301/Samsung-TVs-and-Displays-Samsung-Art-Store-Kunsthistorisches-Museum_main4.jpg" alt="Back row, from left: Manuel Laporta Osante, Head of TV/AV - Samsung Austria, Verena Eisner, Deputy CFO - The Kunsthistorisches Museum Wien, Antonio Marques, Strategy Product Manager - Samsung Austria. Front row, from left: Paul Frey, General Manager - The Kunsthistorisches Museum Wien, Dario Casari, President - Samsung Austria and Samsung Switzerland." class="wp-image-176474" /><figcaption class="wp-element-caption">▲ Back row, from left: Manuel Laporta Osante, Head of TV/AV – Samsung Austria, Verena Eisner, Deputy CFO – The Kunsthistorisches Museum Wien, Antonio Marques, Strategy Product Manager – Samsung Austria. Front row, from left: Paul Frey, General Manager – The Kunsthistorisches Museum Wien, Dario Casari, President – Samsung Austria and Samsung Switzerland.</figcaption></figure>



<hr class="wp-block-separator has-alpha-channel-opacity" />



<h4 class="wp-block-heading"><strong><u>About the Kunsthistorisches Museum</u></strong><br>The Kunsthistorisches Museum in Vienna is one of the world’s most important universal museums. Since its opening in 1891, it has housed the Habsburg collections and showcases artworks ranging from ancient Egyptian and classical antiquities to masterpieces of European painting. The museum houses the world’s largest collection of paintings by Pieter Bruegel the Elder. The museum building itself is an architectural masterpiece and one of the most impressive landmarks along Vienna’s historic Ringstrasse.</h4>



<hr class="wp-block-separator has-alpha-channel-opacity" />


<ol class="wp-block-footnotes"><li id="240b6226-bc1c-48a1-bba5-33906bb554c3">Samsung Art Store subscription required. Subscription fees apply. Artwork availability and collection contents are subject to change at any time without notice or user notification. <a href="#240b6226-bc1c-48a1-bba5-33906bb554c3-link" aria-label="Jump to footnote reference 1"><img src="https://s.w.org/images/core/emoji/16.0.1/72x72/21a9.png" alt="↩" class="wp-smiley" style="height: 1em; max-height: 1em;" />︎</a></li><li id="ea4b573e-6b99-4fdd-991e-8a3272e7b907">Samsung Art Store is available on the following compatible Samsung TV models: 2026 Models: R95H, S99H, QN80H, QN70H, M80H, The Frame, The Frame Pro. 2025 Models: Neo QLED 8K, Neo QLED 4K, The Frame, The Frame Pro, Q8F, and Q7F. <a href="#ea4b573e-6b99-4fdd-991e-8a3272e7b907-link" aria-label="Jump to footnote reference 2"><img src="https://s.w.org/images/core/emoji/16.0.1/72x72/21a9.png" alt="↩" class="wp-smiley" style="height: 1em; max-height: 1em;" />︎</a></li></ol>]]></content:encoded>

<p>Samsung Electronics today announced an exclusive partnership with Activision as the official TV, monitor, soundbar, and Wi-Fi speaker of Call of Duty: Modern Warfare 4, the latest title in one of the world’s largest first-person shooter (FPS) franchises. The collaboration brings Call of Duty together with Samsung’s premium display and connected gaming technologies to give players more ways to experience the title before its official launch.</p>



<p>Call of Duty is a critically acclaimed and award-winning franchise that has become one of gaming’s most recognized intellectual properties. The collaboration marks another step in Samsung’s efforts to expand next-generation gaming experiences across TVs, monitors, soundbars, Wi-Fi speakers, and connected gaming services.</p>



<p>“We are pleased to be the official TV, monitor, soundbar and Wi-Fi speaker partner for Call of Duty: Modern Warfare 4, bringing players a more immersive experience through Samsung’s premium display and audio technologies,” said Hun Lee, Executive Vice President of the Visual Display (VD) Business at Samsung Electronics. “We will continue working with leading developers to strengthen gaming experiences across our display portfolio.”</p>



<p></p>



<h2 class="wp-block-heading"><strong>HDR10+ GAMING Brings Greater Detail to Gameplay</strong></h2>



<p>Samsung and Call of Duty will integrate <strong>HDR10+ GAMING</strong> into Call of Duty: Modern Warfare 4. The technology helps deliver more accurate color and contrast, supporting the immersive visual experience that reflects the creative intent of the developers.</p>



<p>“Samsung’s display and gaming technologies provide an exciting way to experience Call of Duty: Modern Warfare 4,” said Cody Neal, Head of Partnerships at Activision. “Partnering with Samsung allows us to showcase the game with clarity, scale, and responsiveness that complement the intensity at the heart of Call of Duty.”</p>



<p>Modern Warfare 4 will officially support a <strong>32:9 super ultrawide aspect ratio</strong>, allowing Samsung Odyssey G9 users to experience a wider format giving players a broader sense of space in-game.</p>



<p>Samsung will also give consumers around the world more ways to experience Modern Warfare 4 across Samsung TVs, Odyssey monitors, and gaming services.</p>



<p></p>



<h2 class="wp-block-heading"><strong>Samsung Gaming Hub Streaming Call of Duty: NEXT</strong></h2>



<p>As part of the collaboration, <strong>Samsung Gaming Hub</strong> users will be able to watch Call of Duty: NEXT live through Samsung Gaming Hub on Aug. 21 at 9 am PST. Ahead of the event, a tune-in banner promoting Call of Duty: NEXT will be featured in the Samsung TV Gaming Hub masthead.</p>



<p>Samsung will also distribute Beta Early Access codes for Call of Duty: Modern Warfare 4 through the Samsung Promotion app. The codes will give selected players an opportunity to experience the Modern Warfare 4 Beta from Aug. 21-25, before the Open Beta begins, starting Aug. 28 through Sep. 1. Users can check for Beta Early Access code availability and event details on the Samsung TV Gaming Hub.</p>



<p>Samsung Gaming Hub allows users to access major cloud gaming services, including Xbox Cloud Gaming, as well as other game-related content, in one place. With it, users can access supported services and content without switching devices.</p>



<p></p>



<h2 class="wp-block-heading"><strong>Gamescom 2026: Samsung Odyssey To Showcase Speed and Detail of Modern Warfare 4</strong></h2>



<p>Additionally, Samsung will bring Call of Duty: Modern Warfare 4 to its booth for an early hands-on experience during Gamescom 2026, taking place Aug. 26-30 in Cologne, Germany. As one of the world’s leading events for computer and video games, Gamescom brings together players, creators, publishers, and technology brands from across the industry.</p>



<p>At the event, Samsung will provide Call of Duty: Modern Warfare 4 Vault Edition Battle.net game codes to select players who visit its booth. Visitors will also be able to play the highly anticipated title ahead of its official October 23 release on premium Samsung Odyssey gaming monitors. Built for modern gaming, Odyssey monitors give players the visual clarity and smooth performance needed for competitive play and cinematic moments.</p>



<p>For more information, visit <a href="http://www.samsung.com" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer noopener">www.samsung.com</a>.</p>
