									<description><![CDATA[Samsung Electronics today announced the all-new Galaxy Watch Ultra2 and Galaxy Watch9, delivering a new era of effortless health, where users can unlock in-depth, meaningful health insights simply by wearing these devices. To support 24/7 wearability, Samsung delivers powerful innovations that maximize comfort while advancing performance with Galaxy Watch’s largest battery and brightest display ever, […]]]></description>
																<content:encoded><![CDATA[
<p><strong>Samsung Electronics </strong>today announced the all-new <strong>Galaxy Watch Ultra2 </strong>and<strong> Galaxy Watch9</strong>,<sup data-fn="2bea86aa-5588-435e-b413-ce0cf7db5fce" class="fn"><a href="#2bea86aa-5588-435e-b413-ce0cf7db5fce" id="2bea86aa-5588-435e-b413-ce0cf7db5fce-link">1</a></sup> delivering a new era of effortless health, where users can unlock in-depth, meaningful health insights simply by wearing these devices. To support 24/7 wearability, Samsung delivers powerful innovations that maximize comfort while advancing performance with Galaxy Watch’s <strong>largest battery </strong>and<strong> brightest display </strong>ever, as well as the <strong>Snapdragon Wear®</strong> <strong>Elite Platform</strong>.<sup data-fn="877e3f66-75c4-4a2b-80cb-0b0014649635" class="fn"><a href="#877e3f66-75c4-4a2b-80cb-0b0014649635" id="877e3f66-75c4-4a2b-80cb-0b0014649635-link">2</a></sup> Serving as the ultimate gateway to AI-driven health intelligence, this groundbreaking premium lineup transforms complex biometric data into personalized, actionable guidance across the full spectrum of user health.</p>



<figure class="wp-block-image size-full"><img loading="lazy" width="1000" height="750" src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/07/22211303/Samsung-Mobile-Galaxy-Unpacked-July-2026-Galaxy-Watch9-Ultra-Galaxy-Watch9-Launch_Main1.jpg" alt="Galaxy Watch9 and Galaxy Watch Ultra2 feature the Snapdragon Wear® Elite Platform, alongside the lineup's largest battery and brightest display ever." class="wp-image-175710" /><figcaption class="wp-element-caption">▲ Galaxy Watch9 and Galaxy Watch Ultra2 feature the Snapdragon Wear® Elite Platform, alongside the lineup’s largest battery and brightest display ever.</figcaption></figure>



<p>“Samsung is committed to helping people live better. We want to make the shift from reactive treatment to proactive care as seamless as possible. By integrating everyday devices with our global scale, we’re building a deeply connected health experience.” said TM Roh, Chief Executive Officer, President and Head of the Device eXperience (DX) Division at Samsung Electronics. “The new Galaxy Watch Ultra2 and Galaxy Watch9 represent a massive leap forward in Samsung’s health vision. By continuously tracking health data from the wrist, these new watches act as a vigilant health companion, turning daily vital signs into proactive, actionable insights and providing timely alerts.”</p>



<p></p>



<h2 class="wp-block-heading"><strong>Galaxy Watch Ultra2: Fueling Ambitions and Enabling Peak Performance Through Unrivaled Hardware and Specialized Sports Tracking</strong></h2>



<figure class="wp-block-image"><img loading="lazy" width="1000" height="750" src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/07/22211304/Samsung-Mobile-Galaxy-Unpacked-July-2026-Galaxy-Watch9-Ultra-Galaxy-Watch9-Launch_Main2.jpg" alt="The new 47 mm Galaxy Watch Ultra2 in Titanium Gray." class="wp-image-175711" /><figcaption class="wp-element-caption">▲ The new 47 mm Galaxy Watch Ultra2 in Titanium Gray.</figcaption></figure>



<p>Galaxy Watch Ultra2 is Samsung’s most powerful and advanced Galaxy Watch ever. It is built for extreme outdoor adventures and high-performance seekers who demand long-lasting performance, reliable durability, precision tracking and continuous health monitoring in extreme conditions. <em></em></p>



<p>Galaxy Watch Ultra2’s dedicated tracking modes for outdoor sports help users excel and safely push their limits. The <strong>Trail Run</strong><sup data-fn="5d6af59a-40ea-470f-a9d6-3ae67b075c10" class="fn"><a href="#5d6af59a-40ea-470f-a9d6-3ae67b075c10" id="5d6af59a-40ea-470f-a9d6-3ae67b075c10-link">3</a></sup> feature tracks detailed elevation, climbing progress and terrain impact so users can better pace themselves and reduce injury risk. It also provides real-time hydration guidance through <strong>Nutrition Alert</strong>,<sup data-fn="f3291f3e-d290-4b9e-a34d-8c06a8b19967" class="fn"><a href="#f3291f3e-d290-4b9e-a34d-8c06a8b19967" id="f3291f3e-d290-4b9e-a34d-8c06a8b19967-link">4</a></sup> a new addition to the existing Sweat Loss feature. By estimating sweat loss relative to body weight, it provides guidance on when and how much to hydrate, helping users achieve their running goals.</p>



<figure class="wp-block-image size-full"><img loading="lazy" width="1000" height="750" src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/07/22211305/Samsung-Mobile-Galaxy-Unpacked-July-2026-Galaxy-Watch9-Ultra-Galaxy-Watch9-Launch_Main3.jpg" alt="Galaxy Watch Ultra2 provides detailed trail running data on its 5,000-nit, crystal-clear display" class="wp-image-175712" /><figcaption class="wp-element-caption">▲ Galaxy Watch Ultra2 provides detailed trail running data on its 5,000-nit, crystal-clear display</figcaption></figure>



<p>Galaxy Watch Ultra2 also supports professional diving. Rated IP69K, 10 ATM and EN13319 certified,<sup data-fn="12b63eed-f85a-45ca-8377-fd32ec4807ba" class="fn"><a href="#12b63eed-f85a-45ca-8377-fd32ec4807ba" id="12b63eed-f85a-45ca-8377-fd32ec4807ba-link">5</a></sup> it’s not just dust- and water-resistant — it’s dive-ready. The moment the user enters the water, it can automatically track real-time diving data such as depth, time and water temperature right from their wrist.<sup data-fn="56cf4792-6a07-4c19-8f61-bdce721ff23b" class="fn"><a href="#56cf4792-6a07-4c19-8f61-bdce721ff23b" id="56cf4792-6a07-4c19-8f61-bdce721ff23b-link">6</a></sup> Moreover, users can access advanced diving features, including ascent/descent speed tracking and safe dive limits, by using the exclusive Ultra2 <strong>Diving</strong> app expected to be available later this year. Developed with <strong>Mares</strong>,<sup data-fn="f499bfc6-a6bf-4813-b97e-4c75bb839b5b" class="fn"><a href="#f499bfc6-a6bf-4813-b97e-4c75bb839b5b" id="f499bfc6-a6bf-4813-b97e-4c75bb839b5b-link">7</a></sup>the world’s leading diving equipment brand, these features significantly enhance the watch’s diving capabilities.</p>



<figure class="wp-block-image size-full"><img loading="lazy" width="1000" height="750" src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/07/22211307/Samsung-Mobile-Galaxy-Unpacked-July-2026-Galaxy-Watch9-Ultra-Galaxy-Watch9-Launch_Main4.jpg" alt="Once a user enters the water, Galaxy Watch Ultra2 automatically tracks real-time diving data such as depth, time and water temperature." class="wp-image-175713" /><figcaption class="wp-element-caption">▲ Once a user enters the water, Galaxy Watch Ultra2 automatically tracks real-time diving data such as depth, time and water temperature.</figcaption></figure>



<p>To power these pro-level sports health features, Galaxy Watch Ultra2 is built with unprecedented hardware performance. It is equipped with a massive <strong>800 mAh battery </strong>— a 35% increase in capacity compared to the previous Ultra model. Powered by the <strong>Snapdragon Wear</strong> <strong>Elite platform</strong>, it establishes a new benchmark in power, speed and highly accurate GPS tracking for the Ultra line. The expanded display provides the <strong>world’s first smartwatch with 5,000 nits of brightness</strong>,<sup data-fn="3743a7b0-6400-417b-abab-499bcaf64cae" class="fn"><a href="#3743a7b0-6400-417b-abab-499bcaf64cae" id="3743a7b0-6400-417b-abab-499bcaf64cae-link">8</a></sup> making crystal-clear visibility possible even under intense sunlight.</p>



<p>Galaxy Watch Ultra2 retains its <strong>shock-resistant titanium casing</strong> to help ensure rugged durability, while also introducing enhanced comfort and stability for a broader, more diverse range of users. Despite a significantly larger battery, Galaxy Watch Ultra2 achieves an innovative <strong>slimmer</strong><sup data-fn="0c91c428-92c7-4e8c-8c15-2469028b19a8" class="fn"><a href="#0c91c428-92c7-4e8c-8c15-2469028b19a8" id="0c91c428-92c7-4e8c-8c15-2469028b19a8-link">9</a></sup><strong> design</strong> that transitions seamlessly from rugged outdoor and sports activities to everyday lifestyle use. Advanced internal re-engineering significantly reduces the device’s thickness by 12% compared to the previous generation. Coupled with lighter and softer bands that reduce weight, Galaxy Watch Ultra2 delivers 24/7 wearability, highly precise biometric data tracking and exceptional comfort.</p>



<p></p>



<h2 class="wp-block-heading"><strong>Galaxy Watch9: Everyday Wellness With All-Day Comfort and a Lasting Battery</strong></h2>



<figure class="wp-block-image size-full"><img loading="lazy" width="1000" height="750" src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/07/22211309/Samsung-Mobile-Galaxy-Unpacked-July-2026-Galaxy-Watch9-Ultra-Galaxy-Watch9-Launch_Main5.jpg" alt="Galaxy Watch9 is available with different band materials and colors, including soft silicone and lightweight fabric options." class="wp-image-175714" /><figcaption class="wp-element-caption">▲ Galaxy Watch9 is available with different band materials and colors, including soft silicone and lightweight fabric options.</figcaption></figure>



<p>Galaxy Watch9 is the ultimate everyday health companion, designed for wellness-conscious users who want to build healthier habits through effortless, continuous activity and sleep tracking. This versatile watch turns personal biomarkers into actionable health insights, empowering users to effortlessly manage their daily routines by simply wearing it.</p>



<p>To maximize its role as a health companion, Galaxy Watch9 is backed by powerful hardware and an incredibly comfortable design. It features a <strong>lightweight yet</strong> <strong>durable aluminum casing</strong> that preserves Galaxy Watch’s <strong>signature cushion silhouette</strong>, refined specifically for a closer, more natural fit on the wrist. Combined with a wide range of interchangeable, soft-touch bands, Galaxy Watch9 helps ensure unmatched <strong>all-day comfort </strong>— enabling the continuous, 24/7 wear required to help capture accurate and consistent health data.</p>



<p>This exceptional wearability is powered by next-generation internal innovations. The new <strong>Snapdragon Wear Elite platform</strong> gives Galaxy Watch9 a massive boost in speed and efficiency for lag-free, seamless health monitoring. Its efficient <strong>390 mAh battery</strong><sup data-fn="6f2dfec6-864d-49a6-8463-d831407c6234" class="fn"><a href="#6f2dfec6-864d-49a6-8463-d831407c6234" id="6f2dfec6-864d-49a6-8463-d831407c6234-link">10</a></sup> delivers dependable, around-the-clock tracking from day to night. Furthermore, a brilliant <strong>3,000-nit display</strong> supports effortless readability and crystal-clear visibility under any lighting condition, making it easy for users to check their health status at a glance.</p>



<p></p>



<h2 class="wp-block-heading"><strong>AI-Powered Health: Comprehensive and Actionable Health Insights</strong></h2>



<p>At the core of Galaxy Watch Ultra2 and Galaxy Watch9 are Samsung’s AI-powered health features.Galaxy Watch’s BioActive sensor captures continuous biometric data about a user’s lifestyle habits to develop a deep understanding of their health. Galaxy Watch then translates this complex biometric data into simple, proactive recommendations, helping users understand not just what is happening in their bodies, but what to do next.</p>



<p>The new capabilities — available on both Galaxy Watch Ultra2 and Galaxy Watch9 — advance Samsung’s preventive care vision by combining everyday data with years of rigorous clinical research conducted with world-renowned universities and medical institutions. Built on this foundation, Galaxy Watch is designed to deliver more accurate and meaningful health guidance across key pillars including sleep, activity, cardiovascular health and recovery.</p>



<ul class="wp-block-list">
<li><strong>Sleep Apnea feature</strong><sup data-fn="43ec6200-4d43-4ca7-9732-59277f1c828a" class="fn"><a href="#43ec6200-4d43-4ca7-9732-59277f1c828a" id="43ec6200-4d43-4ca7-9732-59277f1c828a-link">11</a></sup>:<strong> </strong>Backed by new FDA clearance and AI algorithms, delivers deeper insights into breathing disruptions during sleep.</li>



<li><strong>Vitals</strong><sup data-fn="d21f4456-4844-4907-92a9-a50d43a4c2b1" class="fn"><a href="#d21f4456-4844-4907-92a9-a50d43a4c2b1" id="d21f4456-4844-4907-92a9-a50d43a4c2b1-link">12</a></sup>: Proactively monitors a user’s personal health baseline during sleep and provides seamless alerts for significant deviations.</li>



<li><strong>Heart Health Score</strong><sup data-fn="f4639553-132e-4531-a317-cc0c82bb5449" class="fn"><a href="#f4639553-132e-4531-a317-cc0c82bb5449" id="f4639553-132e-4531-a317-cc0c82bb5449-link">13</a></sup>:Provides an effortless understanding of cardiovascular wellness through a simple, actionable score and personalized lifestyle recommendations.</li>



<li><strong>Daily Cardio Load</strong><sup data-fn="93d98104-3589-4d99-b6ab-0052e157a409" class="fn"><a href="#93d98104-3589-4d99-b6ab-0052e157a409" id="93d98104-3589-4d99-b6ab-0052e157a409-link">14</a></sup>: Optimizes training through tailored guidance on workout volume and estimated recovery needs.</li>



<li><strong>Fitness Index</strong><sup data-fn="470dfa0f-8dae-46f1-8026-7b1621b6ff50" class="fn"><a href="#470dfa0f-8dae-46f1-8026-7b1621b6ff50" id="470dfa0f-8dae-46f1-8026-7b1621b6ff50-link">15</a></sup>: Delivers a holistic view of physical fitness with personalized goals to improve everyday performance.</li>



<li><strong>Hearing</strong><sup data-fn="d45a01e9-dce4-4a50-ac18-b1863aa8a9a5" class="fn"><a href="#d45a01e9-dce4-4a50-ac18-b1863aa8a9a5" id="d45a01e9-dce4-4a50-ac18-b1863aa8a9a5-link">16</a></sup>: Protects long-term hearing health with alerts for unsafe noise levels and detailed reports.</li>
</ul>



<p>With these features, users can gain access to intelligent, personalized insights that empower them to seamlessly manage their long-term wellness journey and reach their goals.</p>



<figure class="wp-block-image size-full"><img loading="lazy" width="1000" height="750" src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/07/22211311/Samsung-Mobile-Galaxy-Unpacked-July-2026-Galaxy-Watch9-Ultra-Galaxy-Watch9-Launch_Main6.jpg" alt="Galaxy Watch features include Vitals to alert users to significant health deviations." class="wp-image-175715" /><figcaption class="wp-element-caption">▲ Galaxy Watch features include Vitals to alert users to significant health deviations.</figcaption></figure>



<p></p>



<h2 class="wp-block-heading"><strong>Purpose-Built Band Collections: Designed for Every Lifestyle</strong></h2>



<p>A smartwatch should be as unique as the user, and both Galaxy Watch Ultra2 and Galaxy Watch9 offer a range of diverse band options to fit every lifestyle.</p>



<p>Whether users are tackling rugged terrain, diving deep or heading into a business meeting, they have the flexibility to choose the perfect band tailored to their specific purpose and environment, ensuring optimal functionality without compromising on style. Users have the following options to choose from:</p>



<ul class="wp-block-list">
<li><strong>Marine Band:</strong> an even lighter, breathable silicone band designed for all-day adventures and extreme conditions.</li>



<li><strong>Pe</strong><strong>akF</strong><strong>orm Band:</strong> a hybrid material band delivering a premium finish optimized for both elegance and athletic performance.</li>



<li><strong>Trail Band:</strong> a classic fabric band featuring enhanced breathability and a superior fit for lasting comfort, even during demanding outdoor activities like running.</li>
</ul>



<p>Designed with ultimate self-expression in mind, the expansive new Watch9 band collection empowers users to showcase their personal style and customize their look. Featuring innovative new materials and a vibrant spectrum of colors, these bands maximize comfort across diverse activities — from intense workouts to your everyday routines:</p>



<ul class="wp-block-list">
<li><strong>Sports Band:</strong> a thinner, softer all-rounder for ultimate comfort and effortless transitions from workouts to daily life.</li>



<li><strong>Misty Band</strong>: crafted from premium soft silicone that securely wraps around the wrist for a stable, bounce-free fit. Featuring subtle and soft two-tone colors, it delivers a sophisticated look that effortlessly complements a user’s everyday style.</li>



<li><strong>Fabric Band:</strong> a lightweight, breathable daily band offering maximum comfort, even during sleep.</li>
</ul>



<p></p>



<h2 class="wp-block-heading"><strong>Samsung Care+: Quick Access to Diagnostics and More</strong></h2>



<p>To give users peace of mind after upgrading, Samsung Care+<sup data-fn="a9ac8c74-03d1-4be9-93fd-67e02834db7a" class="fn"><a href="#a9ac8c74-03d1-4be9-93fd-67e02834db7a" id="a9ac8c74-03d1-4be9-93fd-67e02834db7a-link">17</a></sup> offers comprehensive protection with fast repairs for accidental damage, extended warranty coverage and certified expert support at home and abroad, making it easier to stay covered wherever life takes them.</p>



<p>Additionally, Galaxy Watch users can manage their Samsung Care+ plan from the Warranty and care menu available on Galaxy mobile phones running on One UI 9. This is a new settings hub that gives users quick access to warranty information, self-diagnostics, repair requests, remote support and Samsung Care+ services — all from their mobile phone.</p>



<p></p>



<h2 class="wp-block-heading"><strong>Galaxy Watch Ultra2 and Galaxy Watch9 come with exclusive subscription trial benefits:</strong></h2>



<ul class="wp-block-list">
<li><strong>Strava 60-Day Free Trial</strong><sup data-fn="0ce0db88-57c3-454f-95fb-d481c5bef23e" class="fn"><a href="#0ce0db88-57c3-454f-95fb-d481c5bef23e" id="0ce0db88-57c3-454f-95fb-d481c5bef23e-link">18</a></sup>:Users canstart training with the world’s most active community, where 195 million active people across 185 countries track every effort and cheer each other on. But Strava goes beyond training — users can connect with their community and discover new running routes by tracking workouts directly on their watch, then opening the Strava app on their mobile phone. To activate a free trial, users can open the Strava mobile app, go to Settings and tap the Samsung offer.</li>



<li><strong>iFIT 2-Month Free Trial</strong><sup data-fn="0a80fbc6-dd9d-4bc0-8371-db1425c6edc9" class="fn"><a href="#0a80fbc6-dd9d-4bc0-8371-db1425c6edc9" id="0a80fbc6-dd9d-4bc0-8371-db1425c6edc9-link">19</a></sup>: Users can explore global destinations with elite iFIT trainers and track workouts directly on their watch. To activate a free trial, users can open the Samsung Health app on their mobile phone and tap promotion in the Settings menu.</li>
</ul>



<p></p>



<h2 class="wp-block-heading"><strong>Availability</strong><sup data-fn="e91a3c8f-238f-4f10-b511-8397480d3e79" class="fn"><a href="#e91a3c8f-238f-4f10-b511-8397480d3e79" id="e91a3c8f-238f-4f10-b511-8397480d3e79-link">20</a></sup></h2>



<p>Together with the new Galaxy Z series, Galaxy Watch Ultra2 and Watch9 expands Samsung’s AI ecosystem, bringing life companion experiences across every form factor, from wrist to pocket to home.</p>



<p>Galaxy Watch Ultra2 will be available in a <strong>47 mm size</strong>, while Galaxy Watch9 will be available in two sizes: <strong>40 mm</strong> and <strong>44 mm</strong>. Galaxy Watch Ultra2 users will be able to choose from <strong>Titanium Silver</strong> or <strong>Titanium Gray</strong>. Meanwhile, the40 mm Galaxy Watch9 will be available in <strong>Cream </strong>and <strong>Graphite</strong>, while the 44 mm Galaxy Watch9 will be available in <strong>Graphite</strong> and <strong>Silver</strong>.</p>



<p>Galaxy Watch Ultra2 and Galaxy Watch9 are available for pre-order in select markets starting July22, with general availability starting Aug 7.</p>



<p>To learn more about Galaxy Watch Ultra2 and Galaxy Watch9, please visit: <a href="https://news.samsung.com/global/" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer noopener">Samsung Global Newsroom</a> or <a href="http://samsung.com/" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer noopener">samsung.com.</a></p>



<p></p>

																<content:encoded><![CDATA[
<p>Samsung Electronics today introduced its intelligent eyewear at Galaxy Unpacked in London. Building on the concepts announced at the latest Google I/O, the new designs were developed with global eyewear brands, Gentle Monster and Warby Parker. The intelligent eyewear shows how the Galaxy ecosystem can move beyond the mobile phone and into eyewear that supports daily routines, work, travel and hands-free moments.</p>



<p></p>



<h2 class="wp-block-heading"><strong>What’s New:</strong></h2>



<ul class="wp-block-list">
<li>Designed for everyday use, the eyewear pairs a lightweight frame and slim profile with the durability and comfort needed for daily wear.</li>



<li>Developed in collaboration with Gentle Monster and Warby Parker, the eyewear reflects each brand’s expertise in design, offering a range of frame styles, colors and lens options to suit different preferences and lifestyles.</li>
</ul>



<p>“As agentic AI reshapes mobile experiences, the next step is to make those experiences more intuitive and responsive to each user’s context,” said Won-Joon Choi, Chief Operating Officer of the Mobile eXperience (MX) Business at Samsung Electronics. “Intelligent eyewear creates a new point of interaction, bringing more personalized AI assistance naturally into the moments that make up everyday life.”</p>



<figure class="wp-block-image size-full"><img loading="lazy" width="1000" height="280" src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/07/22213356/Samsung-Mobile-Galaxy-Unpacked-July-2026-Intelligent-Eyewear-Launch_main1.jpg" alt="Building on the concepts announced at the latest Google I/O, the new designs were developed with global eyewear brands, Gentle Monster and Warby Parker." class="wp-image-175734" /></figure>



<p></p>



<h2 class="wp-block-heading"><strong>Why It Matters</strong></h2>



<p>As AI enters an agentic era, the question is no longer only how intelligent it becomes, but where and how that intelligence meets people. Mobile phones remain at the center of connected experiences, while devices across the Galaxy ecosystem add context to different moments of daily life. Intelligent eyewear represents that next step, bringing AI closer to people as they move, work, communicate and explore, without requiring every interaction to begin with a screen.</p>



<p>Built for these moments, intelligent eyewear brings AI directly into the user’s line of sight, providing timely information and assistance while allowing them to stay engaged with the people, places and tasks around them. Clear controls and safeguards support responsible and secure use, while hands-free access delivers assistance naturally without requiring users to stop what they are doing or turn to another screen.</p>



<p></p>



<h2 class="wp-block-heading"><strong>New Intelligent Eyewear Experience</strong></h2>



<p>Designed for everyday wear, the eyewear combines a lightweight frame, slim profile and battery performance, offering up to nine hours on a single charge with up to seven additional full charges from the charging case.<sup data-fn="c8265e1c-fd48-45f1-92ee-4cdc4570c950" class="fn"><a href="#c8265e1c-fd48-45f1-92ee-4cdc4570c950" id="c8265e1c-fd48-45f1-92ee-4cdc4570c950-link">1</a></sup> A built-in camera brings visual context into the AI experience, while Snapdragon<sup>®</sup> AR1 Gen1<sup data-fn="74a320d8-765e-4713-b202-b88dce3f388f" class="fn"><a href="#74a320d8-765e-4713-b202-b88dce3f388f" id="74a320d8-765e-4713-b202-b88dce3f388f-link">2</a></sup> Platform supports the AI performance designed for real-time assistance, with touch interaction, connectivity, power efficiency and thermal management optimized within the eyewear’s compact frame.</p>



<p>As a hands-free extension of the Galaxy experience, the device lets users stay focused on the moment. Built in partnership with Google, it brings Gemini into a familiar wearable form factor, making interaction feel more natural, immediate and intuitive.</p>



<p>“Google and Samsung have a shared vision to bring helpful and context-aware intelligence into everyday life,” said Sameer Samat, President of Android Ecosystem at Google. “Built on the Android XR platform, the intelligent eyewear blends world-class designs with Gemini’s advanced assistance that naturally responds to what you see, delivering all-day, hands-free help. We’re excited to continue this momentum and bring this experience to people soon.”</p>



<p>Key experiences include:</p>



<ul class="wp-block-list">
<li><strong>Staying in the moment:</strong> The device can help summarize long messages, read key information aloud, support live translation and allow users to control the experience with simple voice or gesture interactions through connected devices.<sup data-fn="fc0a2a1b-8e8f-490e-9748-fdc585db76d5" class="fn"><a href="#fc0a2a1b-8e8f-490e-9748-fdc585db76d5" id="fc0a2a1b-8e8f-490e-9748-fdc585db76d5-link">3</a></sup></li>



<li><strong>Capturing context:</strong> When prompted, the eyewear can capture what the user is seeing, such as a whiteboard or meeting discussion, organize key information in Notes and make it easier to review, share and follow-up on later.<sup data-fn="398d65a6-f9b9-44b9-af19-2caa670df1fc" class="fn"><a href="#398d65a6-f9b9-44b9-af19-2caa670df1fc" id="398d65a6-f9b9-44b9-af19-2caa670df1fc-link">4</a></sup></li>



<li><strong>Real-time interaction:</strong> Users can ask for guidance based on their location, such as finding a route, setting a destination or understanding what is around them, while live translation can support communication in another language.<sup data-fn="fdeba69e-cafd-4949-b997-3dc01aa89dae" class="fn"><a href="#fdeba69e-cafd-4949-b997-3dc01aa89dae" id="fdeba69e-cafd-4949-b997-3dc01aa89dae-link">5</a></sup></li>



<li><strong>Context-aware assistance:</strong> The device can support more useful follow-up interactions by understanding the user’s current context and connecting relevant information across tasks, helping users continue where they left off with less repetition.<sup data-fn="e58b507f-2af9-4435-83f3-655fa4f54d13" class="fn"><a href="#e58b507f-2af9-4435-83f3-655fa4f54d13" id="e58b507f-2af9-4435-83f3-655fa4f54d13-link">6</a></sup></li>



<li><strong>Live visual sharing:</strong> Users can share what they are seeing during a call or record their own point of view, making communication more immediate while keeping their hands free.<sup data-fn="3dbf37f8-30b0-4272-bf04-df17eedcc835" class="fn"><a href="#3dbf37f8-30b0-4272-bf04-df17eedcc835" id="3dbf37f8-30b0-4272-bf04-df17eedcc835-link">7</a></sup></li>
</ul>



<p></p>



<h2 class="wp-block-heading"><strong>A More Personal Approach to Eyewear</strong></h2>



<p>Eyewear is both a functional product and a personal style statement. Samsung worked with Gentle Monster and Warby Parker to create intelligent eyewear that balances thoughtful design, comfort and everyday wearability. By combining Samsung’s hardware craftsmanship with each brand’s distinctive design approach, the collaboration offers a broader range of styles to suit different preferences, lifestyles and occasions.</p>



<p>At the Unpacked, Samsung introduced two distinct designs by: Gentle Monster and Warby Parker.</p>



<p>The Gentle Monster edition features a slim frame in black, pairing a straight upper rim with softly rounded lower edges to create a refined and fashion-forward silhouette. The Warby Parker style features a subtly upswept brow line in a rich brown hue, creating a refined, versatile shape perfect for everyday settings. </p>



<p>Throughout the design process, Samsung worked closely with the eyewear partners to refine key fit points such as the forehead and ears. These insights helped shape the final designs with enhanced comfort and everyday wearability. Fit considerations were tailored to each model, alongside durability and reliable performance.</p>



<figure class="wp-block-image size-full"><img loading="lazy" width="1000" height="396" src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/07/22213445/Samsung-Mobile-Galaxy-Unpacked-July-2026-Intelligent-Eyewear-Launch_main2.jpg" alt="Samsung Electronics today introduced its intelligent eyewear at Galaxy Unpacked in London." class="wp-image-175735" /></figure>



<hr class="wp-block-separator has-alpha-channel-opacity" />


<ol class="wp-block-footnotes"><li id="c8265e1c-fd48-45f1-92ee-4cdc4570c950">Battery life varies by network environment, features and apps used, device settings, the number of times charged and many other factors. <a href="#c8265e1c-fd48-45f1-92ee-4cdc4570c950-link" aria-label="Jump to footnote reference 1"><img src="https://s.w.org/images/core/emoji/16.0.1/72x72/21a9.png" alt="↩" class="wp-smiley" style="height: 1em; max-height: 1em;" />︎</a></li><li id="74a320d8-765e-4713-b202-b88dce3f388f">Snapdragon is a trademark or registered trademark of Qualcomm Incorporated. 																<content:encoded><![CDATA[
<figure class="wp-block-image size-full"><img loading="lazy" width="1000" height="667" src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/07/21060705/Samsung-Mobile-Galaxy-Unpacked-July-2026-Samsung-Galaxy-Card-Samsung-Wallet_Main1.jpg" alt="An image of Samsung Galaxy Card_Z Flip7 Open" class="wp-image-175653" /></figure>



<p><a href="https://www.samsung.com/us/?r=true&referrer=usnewsroom" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer noopener">Samsung Electronics America</a> today unveiled Samsung Galaxy Card, a seamless way to earn cash rewards on everyday purchases with increased cash rewards for purchases made from Samsung — including the latest devices. Samsung’s first ever credit card offers a new financing option, whether you are giving the gift of Galaxy, treating yourself to a new device, or want to earn cash rewards on all your favorite items.</p>



<p>Samsung Galaxy Card is issued by Barclays US Consumer Bank on the Visa network. Starting July 22, 2026, applications can be submitted online, including while shopping at any <a href="https://www.samsung.com/us/store/?r=true&referrer=usnewsroom#find-a-store" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer noopener">Samsung retail</a> store in the U.S., to earn an additional $200 in bonus cash rewards after spending $2,000 in the first 90 days of account opening.</p>



<figure class="wp-block-image size-full"><img loading="lazy" width="1000" height="667" src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/07/21060716/Samsung-Mobile-Galaxy-Unpacked-July-2026-Samsung-Galaxy-Card-Samsung-Wallet_Main2.jpg" alt="" class="wp-image-175654" /></figure>



<p>Samsung Galaxy Card is available as a virtual and premium, metal physical card with a black Samsung logo. Users earn a host of cash rewards and other benefits, including:</p>



<ul class="wp-block-list">
<li>5% cash rewards on eligible purchases made directly with Samsung<sup data-fn="c4566db3-b408-4833-8937-98e121509112" class="fn"><a href="#c4566db3-b408-4833-8937-98e121509112" id="c4566db3-b408-4833-8937-98e121509112-link">1</a></sup></li>



<li>3% cash rewards on purchases made with Samsung Wallet<sup data-fn="6ffd5ed2-0d97-49da-8dc3-8a70c0367c2c" class="fn"><a href="#6ffd5ed2-0d97-49da-8dc3-8a70c0367c2c" id="6ffd5ed2-0d97-49da-8dc3-8a70c0367c2c-link">2</a></sup></li>



<li>2% cash rewards on streaming services, like Netflix, Disney+, and Spotify</li>



<li>1% cash rewards on all other purchases</li>



<li>20% off Samsung VIP Advantage,<sup data-fn="9014c57c-f601-44f5-8cb4-170d4d4b00ab" class="fn"><a href="#9014c57c-f601-44f5-8cb4-170d4d4b00ab" id="9014c57c-f601-44f5-8cb4-170d4d4b00ab-link">3</a></sup> plus earn 5% cash rewards when purchasing or renewing a Samsung VIP Advantage membership. <a href="https://www.barclaycardus.com/applycontent/TnCs.jsp?tc48355" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer noopener">Terms apply</a></li>
</ul>



<p>“For years, users have relied on Samsung products to enhance their daily life — from staying connected with friends, to relaxation, to productivity. Today, Samsung Galaxy Card offers users a new way to be rewarded every time you pay,” said Woncheol Chai, EVP and Head of the Digital Wallet Team at the Mobile eXperience (MX) Business at Samsung Electronics. “Users are busy, they need a credit card that simplifies their life and offers them real value — Samsung Galaxy Card does both.”</p>



<figure class="wp-block-image size-full"><img loading="lazy" width="1000" height="329" src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/07/21060812/Samsung-Mobile-Galaxy-Unpacked-July-2026-Samsung-Galaxy-Card-Samsung-Wallet_Main3.jpg" alt="An image of Samsung Galaxy Z Flip7 and Samsung Galaxy Z Fold7" class="wp-image-175656" /></figure>



<p>With Samsung Galaxy Card’s convenient integration with <a href="https://www.samsung.com/us/support/owners/app/samsung-wallet?r=true&referrer=usnewsroom" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer noopener">Samsung Wallet</a>, users can store it alongside other compatible cards, IDs,<sup data-fn="4a8f096a-854a-4e5f-a42a-ce92f4bdefd6" class="fn"><a href="#4a8f096a-854a-4e5f-a42a-ce92f4bdefd6" id="4a8f096a-854a-4e5f-a42a-ce92f4bdefd6-link">4</a></sup> passes, digital keys,<sup data-fn="19c438b6-84ce-40cc-a84c-aa5337dcec81" class="fn"><a href="#19c438b6-84ce-40cc-a84c-aa5337dcec81" id="19c438b6-84ce-40cc-a84c-aa5337dcec81-link">5</a></sup> and more. <a href="https://www.samsung.com/us/support/owners/app/samsung-wallet?r=true&referrer=usnewsroom" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer noopener">Samsung Wallet</a> is designed to keep what users need within reach while supporting an open ecosystem through trusted partner integrations that expand user experiences over time. Plus, enjoy added peace of mind with Samsung’s on-device defense-grade security platform Samsung Knox.</p>



<p>“Partnering with Samsung is a strong example of Barclays partner-first strategy and the innovation we’re bringing to the market with some of the world’s most recognized brands,” said Denny Nealon, CEO of Barclays US Consumer Bank. “Together, we’re creating seamless, rewarding digital banking capabilities and products that meet customers where they are — in their <a href="https://www.samsung.com/us/support/owners/app/samsung-wallet?r=true&referrer=usnewsroom" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer noopener">Samsung Wallet</a> — and fit naturally into how they live, shop, and connect.”</p>



<p>“Consumers expect payments to be embedded into the digital experiences they use every day,” said Kirk Stuart, SVP, Head of North America Enablers, Merchants and Fintechs at Visa. “By bringing together Samsung’s innovation, Barclays’ co-brand capabilities, and Visa’s trusted network, Samsung Galaxy Card delivers a connected experience that helps consumers earn rewards while providing the security and acceptance needed to support commerce wherever they choose to shop.”</p>



<p>Eligible early access customers can apply online for Samsung Galaxy Card today. <a href="https://www.barclaycardus.com/applycontent/TnCs.jsp?tc48355" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer noopener">Terms apply.</a> General public applications begin July 22, 2026.</p>



<p>Tune into Galaxy Unpacked live on July 22 on <a href="http://www.samsung.com/" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer noopener">Samsung.com</a>, <a href="https://news.samsung.com/global/" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer noopener">Samsung Newsroom</a> and <a href="https://www.youtube.com/samsung" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer noopener">Samsung’s YouTube channel</a> beginning 9 a.m. EDT to learn more about the <a href="https://www.samsung.com/us/smartphones/galaxy-z/" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer noopener">Galaxy Z series</a> and the full Galaxy lineup.</p>

				<title><![CDATA[Samsung Launches Health Assistant Beta, First Fully Integrated AI-Powered Personal Health Assistant]]></title>
				<link>https://news.samsung.com/global/samsung-launches-health-assistant-beta-first-fully-integrated-ai-powered-personal-health-assistant</link>
				<pubDate>Tue, 21 Jul 2026 00:00:00 +0000</pubDate>
								<media:content url="https://news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/themes/newsroom/images/default_image.png" medium="image" />
				<dc:creator><![CDATA[Samsung Newsroom]]></dc:creator>
						<category><![CDATA[Worldwide]]></category>
                <guid isPermaLink="false">https://bit.ly/4wkewFQ</guid>
									<description><![CDATA[Samsung Health Assistant, a fully integrated AI-powered personal health assistant for Samsung Health, is now available in beta for eligible U.S. users. Health Assistant will help users achieve their health and wellness goals by connecting important health data with insights that empower them to ask questions about their health and gain access to rich, personalized […]]]></description>
																<content:encoded><![CDATA[
<p>Samsung Health Assistant, a fully integrated AI-powered personal health assistant for Samsung Health, is now available in beta for eligible U.S. users.</p>



<figure class="wp-block-image size-full"><img loading="lazy" width="1000" height="1000" src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/07/22170138/Samsung-Worldwide-Article-Health-Assistant-Beta_Main1.jpg" alt="" class="wp-image-175673" /></figure>



<p>Health Assistant will help users achieve their health and wellness goals by connecting important health data with insights that empower them to ask questions about their health and gain access to rich, personalized health guidance and recommendations.</p>



<p>Through a holistic understanding of a user’s health, Health Assistant will connect information from Samsung Health’s five core pillars of wellness — Sleep, Activity, Nutrition, Mindfulness and Vitals — and transform this data into meaningful, actionable insights while explaining how each variable impacts the others.</p>



<p>Health Assistant will then identify patterns and signals users might otherwise miss and provide high quality, proactive health tracking recommendations that have been validated by a team of physicians and certified health coaches.</p>



<p>“Our new Health Assistant advances Samsung’s vision for connected care by giving users a deeper understanding of their mind and body through daily health monitoring and increasing opportunities for early detection,” said Dr. Hon Pak, Senior Vice President and Head of the Digital Health Team, Mobile eXperience (MX) Business at Samsung Electronics. “By leveraging our ecosystem and partnerships, we aim to deliver a holistic understanding of health and drive real action, helping users build healthier habits and bringing real change to their lives.”</p>



<p>Samsung’s acquisition of Xealth extends this by connecting everyday wellness data to clinical care — accelerating the ability to connect user-consented health insights with care teams securely and at scale.</p>



<p>Health Assistant’s future capabilities will explore coaching to help users achieve their goals through habit changes and a wider set of services available in Samsung Health, such as weight management.</p>



<p><a href="https://news.samsung.com/us/samsung-launches-health-assistant-beta-first-fully-integrated-ai-powered-assistant/" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer noopener">Learn more at the Samsung U.S. Newsroom</a></p>
]]></content:encoded>

									<description><![CDATA[Many Galaxy users have likely come across a message such as, “One account. Any device. Just for you.” While signing in across devices with a single account may feel familiar, Samsung Account is much more than a login. As a key component of the Samsung ecosystem, it connects products, services and experiences. With Samsung Account, […]]]></description>
																<content:encoded><![CDATA[
<p>Many Galaxy users have likely come across a message such as, “One account. Any device. Just for you.”</p>



<p>While signing in across devices with a single account may feel familiar, Samsung Account is much more than a login. As a key component of the Samsung ecosystem, it connects products, services and experiences.</p>



<figure class="wp-block-image size-full"><img loading="lazy" width="1000" height="562" src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/07/16171957/Samsung-Corporate-Samsung-Account-Samsung-Wallet-Samsung-Find-SmartThings_main1.jpg" alt=" Samsung Account" class="wp-image-175613" /></figure>



<p>With Samsung Account, users can access a wide range of services including Samsung Health, Samsung Wallet, Galaxy Store and Samsung Cloud, as well as connect and control their Samsung devices through SmartThings.</p>



<p>Samsung Newsroom explores how Samsung Account helps make everyday life more convenient and secure.</p>



<p></p>



<h2 class="wp-block-heading"><strong>Seamless Convenience Across Devices and Services</strong></h2>



<figure class="wp-block-image size-full"><img loading="lazy" width="1000" height="574" src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/07/16172019/Samsung-Corporate-Samsung-Account-Samsung-Wallet-Samsung-Find-SmartThings_main2.jpg" alt="With Samsung Account, users can access a wide range of services like Samsung Wallet, Samsung Health, Galaxy Store and Samsung Cloud. " class="wp-image-175614" /></figure>



<p>Across Samsung’s mobile devices, TVs and home appliances, Samsung Account has become a seamless part of everyday life.</p>



<p></p>



<p></p>



<h4 class="wp-block-heading has-medium-font-size"><strong>New Phone, Same Samsung Wallet</strong></h4>



<p>Samsung Wallet,<sup data-fn="68c5c9dd-9b22-420e-b5d4-c966e608b427" class="fn"><a href="#68c5c9dd-9b22-420e-b5d4-c966e608b427" id="68c5c9dd-9b22-420e-b5d4-c966e608b427-link">1</a></sup> powered by Samsung Account, is a popular service among Galaxy users. When switching to a new phone, users can sign in with their Samsung Account to access saved items in Samsung Wallet, such as coupons and boarding passes.</p>



<p></p>



<p></p>



<h4 class="wp-block-heading has-medium-font-size"><strong>Lost Galaxy Buds? No Problem!</strong></h4>



<p>Misplacing a small device like earbuds can be a stressful experience. That’s where Samsung Find<sup data-fn="7df08104-dcb8-4a23-a20a-7eeceec8cfdc" class="fn"><a href="#7df08104-dcb8-4a23-a20a-7eeceec8cfdc" id="7df08104-dcb8-4a23-a20a-7eeceec8cfdc-link">2</a></sup> comes in.</p>



<p>With Galaxy devices connected through Samsung Account, users can locate a missing device — such as Galaxy Buds — using another Galaxy device signed in to the same account.</p>



<p>Samsung Account also helps users protect lost Galaxy smartphones. If a Galaxy smartphone registered to a Samsung Account goes missing, users can activate Lost Mode through Samsung Find to help prevent unauthorized transactions.</p>



<p></p>



<p></p>



<h4 class="wp-block-heading has-medium-font-size"><strong>A Cool Home Before Arrival</strong></h4>



<p>Samsung Account is essential to the SmartThings<sup data-fn="af74656a-93c7-4d07-a517-321f99a98451" class="fn"><a href="#af74656a-93c7-4d07-a517-321f99a98451" id="af74656a-93c7-4d07-a517-321f99a98451-link">3</a></sup> experience, connecting Samsung devices to enhance everyday convenience. On a hot summer day, users with a SmartThings-connected air conditioner can receive a Welcome Air Care<sup data-fn="26605147-7c41-41f7-bbb6-2fff22964d1a" class="fn"><a href="#26605147-7c41-41f7-bbb6-2fff22964d1a" id="26605147-7c41-41f7-bbb6-2fff22964d1a-link">4</a></sup> notification asking whether they want to turn on the air conditioner before arriving home. Features like this are possible because smartphones and air conditioners use the same Samsung Account.</p>



<p></p>



<h2 class="wp-block-heading"><strong>Safe and Secure for Everyone</strong></h2>



<figure class="wp-block-image size-full"><img loading="lazy" width="1000" height="489" src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/07/16172039/Samsung-Corporate-Samsung-Account-Samsung-Wallet-Samsung-Find-SmartThings_main3.jpg" alt="Samsung Account helps users enjoy Samsung products and services with greater peace of mind." class="wp-image-175615" /></figure>



<p>Beyond making daily life more convenient, Samsung Account helps users enjoy Samsung products and services with greater peace of mind.</p>



<p></p>



<p></p>



<h4 class="wp-block-heading has-medium-font-size"><strong><strong>Uninterrupted Service, All Year Round</strong></strong></h4>



<p>Samsung Account is backed by a cross-region failover system that switches service operations to another region in the event of a cloud outage, helping ensure uninterrupted access to Samsung services.</p>



<p></p>



<p></p>



<h4 class="wp-block-heading has-medium-font-size"><strong>Peace of Mind for a Child’s First Smartphone</strong></h4>



<p>Since 2023, Samsung Account has supported child accounts, helping parents manage their children’s smartphone experience. Features such as content restrictions and screen time management encourage healthier digital habits while giving parents greater peace of mind. In addition, Samsung Account supports compliance with child and youth protection regulations around the world. Based on the age information registered with a Samsung Account, parental approval may be required before children can purchase or download paid apps from Galaxy Store to meet local regulatory requirements, creating a safer digital environment for families.</p>



<p></p>



<h2 class="wp-block-heading"><strong>From Individual to Family Experiences</strong></h2>



<figure class="wp-block-image size-full"><img loading="lazy" width="1000" height="603" src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/07/16182015/Samsung-Corporate-Samsung-Account-Samsung-Wallet-Samsung-Find-SmartThings_main4_F.jpg" alt="Samsung Account will continue evolving beyond individual experiences to help families enjoy Samsung devices and services together." class="wp-image-175636" /></figure>



<p>Looking ahead, Samsung Account will continue evolving beyond individual experiences to help families enjoy Samsung devices and services together.</p>



<p>Samsung also plans to continue expanding family-focused features that make it easier to stay connected and care for loved ones, including a shared family album, which lets family group members share photos and videos, and the ability to find family members, which allows them to view each other’s location from a Galaxy smartphone or Galaxy Watch.</p>



<p>Samsung Account will continue to strengthen its role as a trusted daily companion, connecting the Samsung ecosystem, users and their families.</p>



<hr class="wp-block-separator has-alpha-channel-opacity" />


<ol class="wp-block-footnotes"><li id="68c5c9dd-9b22-420e-b5d4-c966e608b427">Available features and services may vary by country or region. <a href="#68c5c9dd-9b22-420e-b5d4-c966e608b427-link" aria-label="Jump to footnote reference 1"><img src="https://s.w.org/images/core/emoji/16.0.1/72x72/21a9.png" alt="↩" class="wp-smiley" style="height: 1em; max-height: 1em;" />︎</a></li><li id="7df08104-dcb8-4a23-a20a-7eeceec8cfdc">Available features and services may vary by country or region. <a href="#7df08104-dcb8-4a23-a20a-7eeceec8cfdc-link" aria-label="Jump to footnote reference 2"><img src="https://s.w.org/images/core/emoji/16.0.1/72x72/21a9.png" alt="↩" class="wp-smiley" style="height: 1em; max-height: 1em;" />︎</a></li><li id="af74656a-93c7-4d07-a517-321f99a98451">Available features and services may vary by country or region. <a href="#af74656a-93c7-4d07-a517-321f99a98451-link" aria-label="Jump to footnote reference 3"><img src="https://s.w.org/images/core/emoji/16.0.1/72x72/21a9.png" alt="↩" class="wp-smiley" style="height: 1em; max-height: 1em;" />︎</a></li><li id="26605147-7c41-41f7-bbb6-2fff22964d1a">Welcome Air Care uses a smartphone’s location to detect when users are approaching home and sends a notification asking whether they want to turn on the air conditioner. 				<title><![CDATA[Samsung Announces Addition of Louvre Collection to Samsung Art Store]]></title>
				<link>https://news.samsung.com/global/samsung-announces-addition-of-louvre-collection-to-samsung-art-store</link>
				<pubDate>Mon, 20 Jul 2026 08:00:00 +0000</pubDate>
								<media:content url="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/07/16175310/Samsung-TVs-and-Displays-Samsung-Art-Store-Louvre-Collection_Thumb932-728x410.jpg" medium="image" />
				<dc:creator><![CDATA[Samsung Newsroom]]></dc:creator>

									<description><![CDATA[Samsung Electronics today announced that 34 brand new artworks from the Louvre’s collection are joining Samsung Art Store for the first time, making a total of 51 masterpieces from one of the world’s most celebrated museums available to Samsung Art Store users worldwide. The collection includes Leonardo da Vinci’s “Mona Lisa,” along with some of […]]]></description>
																<content:encoded><![CDATA[
<figure class="wp-block-image size-full"><img loading="lazy" width="1000" height="667" src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/07/16174647/Samsung-TVs-and-Displays-Samsung-Art-Store-Louvre-Collection_main1.jpg" alt="“Portrait of Lisa Gherardini, wife of Francesco del Giocondo, known as the Mona Lisa or La Gioconda” (1503–1519) by Leonardo da Vinci on The Frame" class="wp-image-175623" /><figcaption class="wp-element-caption">▲ “Portrait of Lisa Gherardini, wife of Francesco del Giocondo, known as the Mona Lisa or La Gioconda” (1503–1519) by Leonardo da Vinci on The Frame</figcaption></figure>



<p>Samsung Electronics today announced that 34 brand new artworks from the Louvre’s collection are joining Samsung Art Store for the first time, making a total of 51 masterpieces from one of the world’s most celebrated museums available to Samsung Art Store users worldwide.<sup data-fn="6e3fa6f4-576f-448b-a172-d6bc672710d1" class="fn"><a href="#6e3fa6f4-576f-448b-a172-d6bc672710d1" id="6e3fa6f4-576f-448b-a172-d6bc672710d1-link">1</a></sup> The collection includes Leonardo da Vinci’s “Mona Lisa,” along with some of the Louvre’s most renowned artworks, offering users unprecedented access to centuries of art through compatible Samsung Art TVs.<sup data-fn="9a487206-c84b-4c45-9678-8527a634f661" class="fn"><a href="#9a487206-c84b-4c45-9678-8527a634f661" id="9a487206-c84b-4c45-9678-8527a634f661-link">2</a></sup></p>



<p>The collection features some of the Louvre’s most celebrated masterpieces, including Eugène Delacroix’s “Liberty Leading the People,” Paolo Veronese’s “The Wedding Feast at Cana,” and Jean-Auguste-Dominique Ingres’ “La Grande Odalisque”. These iconic works are complemented by paintings from major European masters such as Caravaggio and Jacques-Louis David, alongside distinguished examples of French Neoclassical and Academic painting, including Dominique Papety’s “Greek Women at the Fountain” and Nicolas Poussin’s “Orpheus and Eurydice” — representing a major expansion of Samsung’s curated digital gallery.</p>



<figure class="wp-block-image size-full"><img loading="lazy" width="1000" height="667" src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/07/16174830/Samsung-TVs-and-Displays-Samsung-Art-Store-Louvre-Collection_main2.jpg" alt="“Greek Women at the Fountain” (around 1841) by Dominique Papety on Samsung OLED S95H" class="wp-image-175624" /><figcaption class="wp-element-caption">▲ “Greek Women at the Fountain” (around 1841) by Dominique Papety on Samsung OLED S95H<sup data-fn="5d3a5fcd-7931-4a81-8fb2-4b10d311538a" class="fn"><a href="#5d3a5fcd-7931-4a81-8fb2-4b10d311538a" id="5d3a5fcd-7931-4a81-8fb2-4b10d311538a-link">3</a></sup></figcaption></figure>



<p>“The Louvre represents the very best of French artistic and cultural heritage,” said Guillaume Rault, Vice President of Consumer Electronics at Samsung Electronics France. “We are proud to bring these masterpieces beyond museum walls and into the homes of millions around the world through our Samsung Art TVs.”</p>



<p>This partnership with the Louvre further strengthens Samsung Art Store’s growing portfolio of leading museums and cultural institutions. As one of the world’s most visited museums, the Louvre is home to some of history’s most important artistic treasures and with Samsung Art Store expanding access to this iconic museum collection, users can discover and display masterpieces on compatible Samsung TVs and displays.</p>



<p>Samsung Art Store offers subscribers access to a curated library of artworks from leading museums and artists worldwide. Available on Samsung Art TVs, the service enables users to discover over 5,000 artworks and enjoy museum art from the comfort of their home.</p>



<p>The Louvre collection is now available globally through Samsung Art Store.</p>



<hr class="wp-block-separator has-alpha-channel-opacity" />



<h4 class="wp-block-heading"><strong><u>About Samsung Art Store</u></strong><br><a href="https://www.samsung.com/us/tvs/art-tv/?r=true&referrer=usnewsroom" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer noopener">Samsung Art Store</a> is the #1 TV art subscription service globally, available in 115+ countries and growing. It offers access to a vast catalog of 5,000+ artworks from more than 800 artists through collaboration with leading institutions such as The Metropolitan Museum of Art, MoMA and Musée d’Orsay, reacquainting people with art in new, meaningful ways. Samsung Art Store is available across several Samsung TV series, making art from the world’s leading artists, museums and galleries more accessible than ever.</h4>



<p></p>



<h4 class="wp-block-heading"><strong><u>About the Musée du Louvre</u></strong><br>Open to all since 1793, the Musée du Louvre was the first museum to open to the general public in France. Born of the French Revolution and heir to the great royal collections, this former palace of the kings of France has always lived and evolved alongside national – and global – history.<br><br>Today, the Louvre is one of the leading players on the international museum scene, with its 30,000 works of art displayed across 70,000 square meters, including masterpieces like the “Mona Lisa,” the “Winged Victory of Samothrace,” the “Seated Scribe,” and the “Venus de Milo.” It holds collections that tell a history of the world that has always been built on exchanges and connections, spanning from antiquity to the 19th century, from Asia to the Americas.<br><br>The Louvre is a museum with a universal mission, a place where cultures and civilizations come together, where past and present interact. It is the place where all kinds of arts and all forms of expression manifest in today’s world – a place to better grasp the very aspirations of humanity.</h4>



<hr class="wp-block-separator has-alpha-channel-opacity" />

				<title><![CDATA[Samsung Launches New Slide-in Ranges and Over-the-Range Microwave With Air Fry Max]]></title>
				<link>https://news.samsung.com/global/samsung-launches-new-slide-in-ranges-and-over-the-range-microwave-with-air-fry-max</link>
				<pubDate>Thu, 16 Jul 2026 08:00:00 +0000</pubDate>
								<media:content url="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/07/15143121/Samsung-Digital-Appliances-Slide-in-Ranges-and-Over-the-Range-Microwave-Air-Fry-Max_Thumb932-728x410.jpg" medium="image" />
				<dc:creator><![CDATA[Samsung Newsroom]]></dc:creator>
						<category><![CDATA[Home Appliances]]></category>
		<category><![CDATA[Press Releases]]></category>
		<category><![CDATA[Air Fry Max]]></category>
		<category><![CDATA[Over-the-Range Microwave]]></category>
		<category><![CDATA[Slide-in Ranges]]></category>
		<category><![CDATA[SmartThings]]></category>
                <guid isPermaLink="false">https://bit.ly/451dSRt</guid>
									<description><![CDATA[Samsung Electronics today announced its new Slide-in Ranges and Over-the-Range Microwave with Air Fry Max, bringing together advanced cooking technologies, premium design and connected experiences to elevate everyday cooking. Available in both electric and gas options, the new Samsung Slide-in Ranges are designed to help consumers prepare healthier meals, achieve consistent cooking results and create […]]]></description>
																<content:encoded><![CDATA[
<figure class="wp-block-image size-full"><img loading="lazy" width="1000" height="563" src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/07/15143219/Samsung-Digital-Appliances-Slide-in-Ranges-and-Over-the-Range-Microwave-Air-Fry-Max_main1.jpg" alt="Samsung Electronics' new Slide-in Ranges and Over-the-Range Microwave with Air Fry Max" class="wp-image-175588" /></figure>



<p>Samsung Electronics today announced its new Slide-in Ranges<sup data-fn="17e3660e-8a37-425f-b10a-557a2e20717f" class="fn"><a href="#17e3660e-8a37-425f-b10a-557a2e20717f" id="17e3660e-8a37-425f-b10a-557a2e20717f-link">1</a></sup> and Over-the-Range Microwave<sup data-fn="58711a09-cb43-45f2-a141-5b021624f5e2" class="fn"><a href="#58711a09-cb43-45f2-a141-5b021624f5e2" id="58711a09-cb43-45f2-a141-5b021624f5e2-link">2</a></sup> with Air Fry Max, bringing together advanced cooking technologies, premium design and connected experiences to elevate everyday cooking. Available in both electric and gas options, the new Samsung Slide-in Ranges are designed to help consumers prepare healthier meals,<sup data-fn="5d0de762-2267-4ef8-8e9a-354f46736e78" class="fn"><a href="#5d0de762-2267-4ef8-8e9a-354f46736e78" id="5d0de762-2267-4ef8-8e9a-354f46736e78-link">3</a></sup> achieve consistent cooking results and create a more refined kitchen environment.</p>



<p>“Consumers today are looking for kitchen appliances that both make cooking easier and complement the way they live and entertain at home,” said Hyoung Min Park, VP and Head of Customer Experience Team for the Digital Appliances (DA) Business at Samsung Electronics. “Whether preparing a quick weekday meal or cooking for family and guests, Samsung’s new Slide-in Ranges and Over-the-Range Microwave with Air Fry Max elevate cooking and make the entire process more seamless.”</p>



<p></p>



<h2 class="wp-block-heading"><strong>Crispy, Healthier Cooking Achieved With Greater Ease</strong></h2>



<p>The new Slide-in Ranges make it easier to prepare crispy, delicious meals with less effort — thanks to Air Fry Max. Designed to deliver crisp, golden results in less time<sup data-fn="8ab84c52-1a62-4ea4-8757-d9ad374d0ed2" class="fn"><a href="#8ab84c52-1a62-4ea4-8757-d9ad374d0ed2" id="8ab84c52-1a62-4ea4-8757-d9ad374d0ed2-link">4</a></sup> without preheating, Air Fry Max helps consumers prepare everyday meals with ease. High temperatures and powerful convection fans deliver the crunch and taste consumers love while also helping to reduce cooking time.</p>



<p>Additionally, the ranges feature True Convection for faster, more even baking and Air Sous Vide,<sup data-fn="b279241f-e0ad-44c5-a3d1-8e05d8619d5a" class="fn"><a href="#b279241f-e0ad-44c5-a3d1-8e05d8619d5a" id="b279241f-e0ad-44c5-a3d1-8e05d8619d5a-link">5</a></sup> which uses precise heat and airflow to help lock in flavor and moisture for gourmet cooking results at home.</p>



<p>To accommodate different cooking preferences, Samsung is introducing both electric and gas models of the Slide-in Range. The electric ranges feature a 3.6 kW Express Boil<img src="https://s.w.org/images/core/emoji/16.0.1/72x72/2122.png" alt="™" class="wp-smiley" style="height: 1em; max-height: 1em;" /> cooktop with dual-ring elements that help accommodate a variety of cookware sizes, while the gas models come with a powerful 23 K BTU Dual Power Burner that delivers fast, high-heat cooking for tasks such as sautéing, searing and boiling.</p>



<p></p>



<h2 class="wp-block-heading"><strong>A More Cohesive Kitchen Aesthetic</strong></h2>



<p>The new Slide-in Ranges combine premium design details with intuitive usability. Featuring the same handle design found on Samsung’s 2026 refrigerator lineup, they help create a cohesive, unified aesthetic throughout the kitchen.</p>



<p>Additionally, Illuminated Precision Knobs for Slide-in Ranges provide clear visual feedback by lighting up when the cooktop is in use, helping consumers instantly see when the cooktop is on. The built-in display is designed to be intuitive and user-friendly, as well, making it easier to view and adjust cooking settings. This results in enhanced usability in the kitchen.</p>



<p>Samsung also brings a premium touch to everyday interactions through its Soft Close Door for Slide-in Ranges. This feature allows the door to close smoothly and quietly, offering a seamless and refined experience in the kitchen. Whether users push the door shut with a foot or close it firmly with one hand, the mechanism softly catches the door and guides it shut. The functionality provided by Soft Close Door is similar to soft-close features found in premium automobiles, bringing elegance and ease to users while they cook.</p>



<p></p>



<h2 class="wp-block-heading"><strong>More Efficient Cooking With Appliances Designed To Work Together</strong></h2>



<p>Samsung’s new Over-the-Range Microwave with Air Fry Max is designed to complement the Slide-in Ranges both functionally and aesthetically.</p>



<p>With Air Fry Max available on both the microwave and the ranges, consumers can prepare larger meals or cook multiple air-fried dishes at once. The feature delivers crispy, healthier results up to 15% faster,<sup data-fn="9105768b-36ce-464e-b82a-beb4bfa51044" class="fn"><a href="#9105768b-36ce-464e-b82a-beb4bfa51044" id="9105768b-36ce-464e-b82a-beb4bfa51044-link">6</a></sup> while Convection Cooking circulates heat to maintain a consistent temperature throughout the cavity, which helps cook meals faster<sup data-fn="02ae07ea-9d61-4a53-a24d-ef5f39597179" class="fn"><a href="#02ae07ea-9d61-4a53-a24d-ef5f39597179" id="02ae07ea-9d61-4a53-a24d-ef5f39597179-link">7</a></sup> and more evenly.</p>



<p>The Slide-in Range and Over-the-Range Microwave also work together through Auto Connectivity,<sup data-fn="98984277-dae3-438d-b646-24cf32ab5baa" class="fn"><a href="#98984277-dae3-438d-b646-24cf32ab5baa" id="98984277-dae3-438d-b646-24cf32ab5baa-link">8</a></sup> which wirelessly syncs the two appliances for an intuitive and convenient cooking experience. When the cooktop is turned on, for example, the Microwave automatically activates its light and ventilation hood. This helps improve visibility and airflow while reducing the need for manual adjustments.</p>



<p>Furthermore, the Over-the-Range Microwave features a slanted, ergonomic design that provides clear visibility to the cooktop while seamlessly blending in with the rest of the kitchen.</p>



<p></p>



<h2 class="wp-block-heading"><strong>Staying Connected To Cooking With SmartThings</strong></h2>



<p>Both the Slide-in Ranges and Over-the-Range Microwave can be connected through SmartThings.<sup data-fn="cb0c6b3b-1ffb-4eb7-88bd-99ffd7d78cd8" class="fn"><a href="#cb0c6b3b-1ffb-4eb7-88bd-99ffd7d78cd8" id="cb0c6b3b-1ffb-4eb7-88bd-99ffd7d78cd8-link">9</a></sup> This allows consumers to conveniently control, monitor and manage these appliances from their smartphone or with a voice assistant<sup data-fn="11cdce73-8771-44f3-8801-319e00421f1f" class="fn"><a href="#11cdce73-8771-44f3-8801-319e00421f1f" id="11cdce73-8771-44f3-8801-319e00421f1f-link">10</a></sup> — while also helping them discover recipes<sup data-fn="0f3296b4-fa73-4fbd-93ad-436f4ddf0806" class="fn"><a href="#0f3296b4-fa73-4fbd-93ad-436f4ddf0806" id="0f3296b4-fa73-4fbd-93ad-436f4ddf0806-link">11</a></sup> and plan meals more easily<sup data-fn="a9dd3b53-b907-4afb-9102-3920dccfd11e" class="fn"><a href="#a9dd3b53-b907-4afb-9102-3920dccfd11e" id="a9dd3b53-b907-4afb-9102-3920dccfd11e-link">12</a></sup> using SmartThings Food.</p>



<p>The new cooking lineup will begin rolling out across North America in mid-July. Among the lineup, the Slide-in Ranges will also be available in select Latin American markets to follow.</p>



<hr class="wp-block-separator has-alpha-channel-opacity" />

				<title><![CDATA[Samsung Introduces Flex Titanium Technology To Advance Foldable Displays]]></title>
				<link>https://news.samsung.com/global/samsung-introduces-flex-titanium-technology-to-advance-foldable-displays</link>
				<pubDate>Wed, 15 Jul 2026 08:00:00 +0000</pubDate>
								<media:content url="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/07/15071833/Samsung-Mobile-Flex-Titanium-Technology-Foldable-Displays_Thumb932-728x410.jpg" medium="image" />
				<dc:creator><![CDATA[Samsung Newsroom]]></dc:creator>
						<category><![CDATA[Mobile]]></category>
		<category><![CDATA[Press Releases]]></category>
		<category><![CDATA[Flex Titanium Technology]]></category>
		<category><![CDATA[Foldable]]></category>
		<category><![CDATA[Galaxy Unpacked July 2026]]></category>
                <guid isPermaLink="false">https://bit.ly/4gxKVnl</guid>
									<description><![CDATA[Samsung Electronics today unveiled new Flex Titanium technology for the next generation of Galaxy foldable devices. Built on seven generations of foldable innovation and engineering expertise, the redesigned display structure utilizes structural and material advancements to deliver a more refined viewing experience with enhanced durability and reduced crease visibility. Made possible thanks to Samsung’s accumulated […]]]></description>
																<content:encoded><![CDATA[
<p>Samsung Electronics today unveiled new Flex Titanium technology for the next generation of Galaxy foldable devices. Built on seven generations of foldable innovation and engineering expertise, the redesigned display structure utilizes structural and material advancements to deliver a more refined viewing experience with enhanced durability and reduced crease visibility. Made possible thanks to Samsung’s accumulated display know-how, Flex Titanium technology integrates two titanium-based components — a titanium-alloy film and a titanium plate — working together to balance slimness, flexibility and strength within the foldable display structure.</p>



<p>“Samsung’s strength in the foldable category comes from connecting user needs with our technologies that deliver tangible benefits in everyday life,” said Sunghoon Moon, EVP and Senior Executive, Mobile R&D Office – H/W, Samsung Electronics. “For the next generation of Galaxy foldables, Samsung is building on years of expertise to bring display innovations into devices that enhance user experiences, anchored by exceptional viewing experiences.”</p>



<p></p>



<h2 class="wp-block-heading"><strong>Building on Years of Foldable Experience</strong></h2>



<p>Samsung has played a leading role in advancing mobile display innovation and bringing it to consumers at scale through mobile devices. This legacy includes the widespread adoption of AMOLED displays in 2007 and the evolution of displays into flexible form factors.</p>



<p>Innovative technologies can only reach their full potential and provide meaningful user experiences through continuous refinement in real-world conditions and the foldable category is no exception. Across seven generations of Galaxy foldables, Samsung has listened closely to consumer feedback. This has given the company a deep understanding of what users expect from the category: a larger, more immersive viewing experience with less visible crease — without compromising durability or portability.</p>



<p>To meet these expectations, Samsung had to rethink how foldable displays are constructed by bringing together breakthroughs in technology, materials and mechanical engineering. The result is the latest milestone in foldable display innovation: Flex Titanium technology. By successfully integrating display innovation with device engineering, Samsung will continue to shape the direction of the mobile industry and deliver standout user experiences.</p>



<p></p>



<h2 class="wp-block-heading">Flex Titanium: Next-Level Viewing</h2>



<p>To create a display that provides an immersive viewing experience while maintaining its ability to withstand everyday use, Samsung took a new approach to structural design. This required a material strong enough to withstand external shock, flexible enough to endure repeated folding and thin enough to fit within a refined device structure. Balancing all three factors was essential to advancing foldable displays — and titanium offered the right mix.</p>



<p>Known for its resilience and strength, titanium has been used in some of the most demanding applications, including satellite antennas and the wheels of a Mars rover. While its strength makes titanium a dependable material, it also presents significant engineering challenges when applied to the thin and flexible structure of a foldable display due to its stiffness. Drawing on its expertise, Samsung has integrated titanium into two key components: a titanium-alloy film and a titanium plate.</p>



<p>The titanium-alloy film is a component that supports the display from within, sitting below the OLED panel. Compared with polymer film, it provides 20 times greater mechanical stiffness. A precision rolling process makes the material exceptionally thin — measuring roughly one-third the thickness of an average human hair — enabling a slimmer display panel.</p>



<p>Below this sits a robust titanium plate, a flexible structure that supports the display module from beneath. It allows tighter bonding with the display module by eliminating air gaps between the module and the adhesive on the plate through advanced hole processing technology. This new structure provides more stable support underneath the display when unfolded while retaining the flexibility needed to accommodate repeated folding.</p>



<p>Furthermore, by integrating a high-resolution architecture and next-generation organic materials, the new display delivers ultra-vivid display resolution while reducing power consumption — delivering a dramatic boost in overall power efficiency.</p>



<p>“By introducing sophisticated micro-patterned holes to the folding section of the titanium plate, we have successfully secured flexibility with robust durability,” said Kyung-Jin Yoo, EVP and Head of Mobile Display Product Development Team, Samsung Display. “Combining high-resolution display architecture with new organic materials that maximize power efficiency, we will further strengthen the competitiveness of next-generation Galaxy foldable devices.”</p>



<p>Together, these advancements enable a strong foldable display that maximizes content immersion on a seamless screen and reduces crease visibility — all while keeping it slim. By balancing strength, flexibility and structural stability, Samsung continues to set the bar for foldable displays.</p>



<p>The new Flex Titanium technology will debut with Samsung’s next-generation Galaxy foldable devices. Further details will be unveiled at <a href="https://news.samsung.com/global/invitation-galaxy-unpacked-july-2026-a-new-shape-unfolds" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer noopener">Galaxy Unpacked</a>.</p>



<figure class="wp-block-image size-full"><img loading="lazy" width="1000" height="563" src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/07/15071722/Samsung-Mobile-Flex-Titanium-Technology-Foldable-Displays_Main1.jpg" alt="The picture of Flex Titanium" class="wp-image-175572" /></figure>
]]></content:encoded>
